Praying that your mercy will allow us to enter Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s abode,  
You’re an outstanding soul who had the wonderful ability  
To preach Kṛṣṇa’s name with complete humility.  
To gain self-realization is what you wanted the world to see.  
We love you dearly, your indebted servants in Coventry.

*Your servants in Coventry, UK.*

## Curitiba

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, who has brought to us the whole of Vṛndāvana-dhāma inside his small traveling luggage! In 1965 the Western world could barely in New York. On account of his infinite mercy we remember his perfect and lovely teachings. Each and every day he delivers thousands of lost lives around the world with his infinite blessings.

Perhaps he is now playing with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, as a little cowherd boy, or he is serving Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. We, his innumerable disciples, worship him, for he has saved us from the miseries of this material world by teaching us the glories proclaimed by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and thus giving us the opportunity to go back home, back to Godhead.

Patita Pāvana Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!!

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants at the ISKCON center in Curitiba, Brazil.  
(written by Gaurasundara dāsa)

## Czech Republic

Our dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances at your lotus feet.

On the *Jaladuta* in 1965, at Boston Harbor, U.S.A., you ended your poem *Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma* with this prayer to Kṛṣṇa: *“bhaktivedānta” nām ebe sārthak koro.* “I have been designated as Bhaktivedānta, and now, if You like, You can fulfill the real purport of Bhaktivedānta.” In the Samsad Bengali-English dictionary, the term *sārthak-nām* is defined as “one who has fully justified one’s name (esp. by one’s attainments and deeds).” So, with the blessings of the Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Himself, this has certainly become a suitable predicate for you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You gave us a practical way out of the established materialistic machinery of the modern society. But you did much more: you outlined a blueprint for changing modern society for the better; to all kinds of seekers you gave the ultimate answers; you set the standard of sanity for everyone; you explained how to really care for others and help them (*janma sārthak kori’ koro para-upakāra*), which you yourself did so abundantly. Thanks to you, so many have changed their lives from nonsensical frustration to meaningful happiness. Kali-yuga is still raging on terribly all around us, but your books and your example provide a beacon emanating a bright light that leads everybody who follows it to a bright future. Even in the most unlikely candidates you are awakening the sense of love of Godhead, the real purport of Bhaktivedanta.

May we always be free from envy and thus able to carry on your mission under the guidance of your faithful servants and in cooperation with other devotees. May we learn more and more to be as personal and pure as you are. May more and more people find their own true nature as *kṛṣṇera ‘nitya-dāsa’* and joyfully glorify your sublime character. Thank you for giving us your precious association.

Your grateful and endeavoring spiritual offspring in the Czech yatra.  
(written by Puṇya Pālaka dāsa)

## Dallas

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, but my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“By the mercy of the guru, even a dumb man can become the greatest orator, and even a lame man can cross mountains.”**

**Eternal Gratitude**

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this, the most auspicious and holy occasion of your 108th Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, please accept the most prostrated *sarvāṅga-daṇḍavat praṇāmas* that our heart can command at the dust of Your Divine Grace’s most magnanimous and divine lotus feet.

All glories to Your Divine Grace, and all glories to your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, and all glories to your ISKCON, all nondifferent and yet full of variegatedness.

We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your many blessings. We could not possibly count every one of them, even if we collectively tried to do so for a long time. Please accept the following admittedly meager attempt.

We thank you for being so exemplary, for giving everything you possessed—all your energy, resources, time, etc.—while brilliantly pushing forward Lord Caitanya’s mission, and for admitting so many into the most powerful spiritual heritage (*paramparā*) alive. You gave everyone so much love, risking your health, home—everything—to give *kṛṣṇa-prema,* the last word in the Supreme Absolute Truth. Your loving concern for everyone you met is perhaps the most important example you set for us.

We thank you for giving us temples, each of which is an oasis in the desert of material existence, and for the Deities in your ISKCON temples, including the most merciful, supermost exquisitely beautiful and utmost munificent Deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī (Kālachandjī is the oldest Deity personally installed by you anywhere). You personally installed these Deities in Dallas and have allowed us to worship Them gorgeously, and also inspired us to appropriately augment Their worship with the Tulasī temple.

We thank you for your writings, which, while articulating the timeless wisdom of *bhakti* and the super-nectarean instructions of Lord Caitanya, which are meant for everyone’s ultimate success, leave spiritual and religious principles unchanged, in accordance with the teachings of predecessor *ācāryas*. Yet, as if miraculously, you have shown us how to faithfully apply these teachings according to time, place, circumstance, and person.

We thank you for your ISKCON, your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, the wonderful association of your disciples, including the wonderful spiritual masters who are instructing us. If anyone reads your books without their help, they will not realize even a very small fraction of a very small fraction of what they can realize. The association of your devotees, including congregational members, is also giving us comradeship and strength in the war against Māyā. Thank you for our GBC and other devotees who are in one way or another always guiding and encouraging us, whom we accept as our authority.

We thank you for engaging us daily in *japa, kīrtana,* and *saṅkīrtana* of the Lord’s Holy Names. By bathing in pure spiritual sound daily, we may easily approach the Supreme Lord and reawaken our natural and unalloyed loving devotional service attitude.

We thank you for the supremely relishable *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*, the honoring of which always keeps us engaged in Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a way nothing else can. And thank you for allowing us to freely distribute *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* through your Food for Life program, festivals, other programs, and the medium of Kālachandji’s Restaurant, which has won valuable awards for the last six years in a row.

We thank you for your *mūrti* in the temple, which allows everyone to still serve you personally, and for your enlightening instructions (*vāṇī*), which are our very life and soul. Your *mūrti* is very important, for it helps everyone feel your presence.

We thank you for showering us with your blessings and mercy, which enable us to perform so many varieties of devotional service, such as serving devotees, *jīva-daya,* Deity worship, *harināma,* festivals, Food for Life, book distribution, teaching, preaching, chanting, feasting and dancing, home programs, other programs, hosting programs like the GBC/Temple Presidents’ meeting, etc. Actually, your blessings and great mercy are all we are made of, and we cannot live without them.

We thank you for the exquisitely beautiful landmark of His Holiness Tamāl Krishna Goswami Mahārāja’s *puṣpa-samādhi,* which is inspiring everyone in so many wonderful ways, just as he continues to do.

We thank you for the wonderful community you have blessed us with, complete with our delightful children and a community school (TKG Academy), within which we apply your teachings to inspire love and trust, tolerating interpersonal differences.

We thank you *ananta-koṭi* times, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are more dear than our own lives. Please bless us that we may always please you and never displease you.

Eternally let us declare “Aṣṭottaraśata Śrī Śrīmad Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!”

*Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī dhāma, in Dallas, along with your servants in North Texas, Oklahoma, and Arkansas.*  
(written by Nityānanda dāsa)

## Denver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on this auspicious day of your appearance celebration.

Your glories are as unfathomable as the ocean, and for one to try to describe them fully is as futile as one’s attempt to swim across the ocean. But we can take a drop from that ocean and begin to understand some of your qualities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have changed all of our lives in so many ways by selflessly giving us your mercy. As Kṛṣṇa has so many attractive features and given so many ways to serve Him, so each of us has found attraction to serving Him through you.

Our beautiful Deities—Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda; Lord Jagannātha, Śrīmatī Subhadrā, and Lord Balarāma; and Gaura-Nitāi—continue to be served faithfully, each and every day, by a wonderful group of devotees. They are all so sincere and dedicated to personally serving the Deity with love and devotion.

Following our annual tradition, we offered the Deities new outfits this year. Lord Jagannātha went out to distribute His mercy to tens of thousands of people at an annual parade here in Denver, plus at a much smaller and intimate gathering for His Ratha-yātrā celebration.

Our Sunday public feast program continues to attract 125–150 people weekly, and each week brings some guests who are visiting your temple for the first time. And the congregation continues to support these programs both financially and through their continued attendance.

You have many times stated that book distribution is very dear to you. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are happy to inform you that in addition to our dedicated full-time book distributors, we have five additional devotees who serve you and Kṛṣṇa by regularly distributing your books.

We also are pleased to inform you that eight devotees have joined our small temple within the last year.

We have a wonderful team of very dedicated devotees who continue to serve the public through our Govinda’s Buffet. We constantly meet people who said they’ve been to Govinda’s to enjoy His wonderful *prasādam*. This is a most satisfying service.

We have begun a weekly program at a very large university downtown. While it is small, it continues to grow, both in attendance and appreciation.

Our Friday evening *harināma* program continues weekly, allowing countless souls to hear the Holy Names and see the devotees.

We regularly have weekly home programs, and the number of devotees hosting these inspiring programs has increased to five.

A very sincere family has moved to Boulder, and they have started a home program there, which has attracted several new guests, who now come regularly to our Denver Sunday programs too.

We have started to have *bhajans* in two locales weekly—on campus and in a restaurant. These have been very favorably received.

And, some devotees have begun a Food for Life program, in addition to our Govinda’s Buffet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, while these activities are very insignificant in their scope, they do show how much we love and appreciate you.

Our prayer to you is that you continue to allow us to serve you in ever-increasing ways and help to maintain all that you have given us.

We hope we have pleased you with our tiny but sincere efforts.

Sincerely,  
Your devotees in Denver, Colorado, USA.

## Eastern Cape

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

This is a very special, auspicious year for ISKCON devotees all over the world. We say this because it is the 108th anniversary of your transcendental appearance. The Society’s leaders the world over are contemplating ways to express their appreciation for your bringing them to the shelter of the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

In Śrīdhām Māyāpur, Your Divine Grace requested us to build a beautiful temple for Their Lordships Rādhā-Mādhava and the Pañca-tattva so that devotees from all over the world could come together to glorify their Lordships during Gaura Pūrṇimā. This is because the Pañca-tattva are *the* inspiration for devotees of this age.

*punaḥ punaḥ piyāiyā haya mahāmatta*  
*nāce, kānde, hāse, gāya, yaiche mada-matta*

**“Śrī Pañca-tattva themselves danced again and again and thus made it easier to drink nectarean love of Godhead. They danced, cried, laughed, and chanted like madmen, and in this way they distributed love of Godhead.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **7.22)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, during the Gaura-Pūrṇimā festival this year I frequently remembered a famous prediction that an astrologer made when he saw you in Māyāpur. He commented that you would build a house in which the whole world can live. And this year we witnessed this with our own eyes, when an estimated 10,000 devotees from all over the world came to Māyāpur to witness the installation of the life-size Pañca-tattva deities. Had you not appeared in this world and established this wonderful movement, ISKCON, this celebration would not have been possible. Your desire was to serve your Guru Mahārāja by preaching the message of Lord Gaurāṅga around the world, and you envisioned a huge temple to glorify the Pañca-tattva at the birthplace of Lord Caitanya, Māyāpur, where you made your ISKCON headquarters. This festival was spiritually surcharged with a glorious *abhiṣeka,* continuous, melodious, and unrelenting *kīrtana,* and wonderful dancing.Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura had a vision of a magnificent temple rising at Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s birthplace, and you instructed your leaders that we should build a very beautiful temple there. When Lord Gaurāṅga was in Purī, He called upon Lord Nityānanda Prabhu to take the responsibility of spreading *harināma saṅkīrtana* in Bengal. While Lord Nityānanda Prabhu was in Bengal, He gave Śrīla Jīva Gosvāmī a tour of Navadvīpa and predicted:

*punaḥ prabhu-icchā ha’ye balavān*  
*ha’be māyāpure ei rūpa vāsa-sthāna*  
*ei saba ghāṭa gaṅgā-tire punaḥ ha’be*  
*prabhura mandira karibena bhakta sabe*  
*adbhuta mandira eka haibe prakāśa*  
*gaurāṅgera nityasevā haibe vikāśa*

**“When again the desire of the Lord manifests strongly, Māyāpur will be a residence for His Deity form. All the bathing** ***ghāṭas*** **will reappear, and the devotees will build the Lord’s temple. An astonishing temple will manifest, and the eternal service to Gaurāṅga will blossom.”**

When you visited South Africa in 1975, you instructed the devotees to build in Durban a temple like the Krishna-Balarām Mandir in Vṛndāvana and to hold a Ratha-yātrā festival. As we know, the Supreme Lord always fulfills the desires of His pure devotee. Therefore, by your mercy devotees built the very beautiful temple you wanted. Since we opened this temple, so many people have visited from all over the world, and here in South Africa people are always talking about this majestic, palatial temple. We are so appreciative to you for these two special instructions. Now we can see with our own eyes exactly why you gave us those instructions.

Outside of India, the yearly Ratha-yātrā festival on the Durban beach is by far the biggest and most successful event of its kind in ISKCON. Every year we distribute thousands of plates of *prasādam* through Food for Life. This festival keeps growing every year, in magnitude as well as opulence. We attract so many *sannyāsīs,* GBCs, and other leaders to this four-day event. You knew the potency of this project.

Today we celebrate your 108th appearance day, and truly we are very privileged to be around to do so. We have made several attempts to get this East Cape chapter off the ground, and at last, with your divine mercy, it is now happening. Land has been donated to your society, and plans are afoot to build a temple. We sincerely pray that you bestow upon us your causeless mercy to achieve this mission in the shortest possible time. We know that to please Your Divine Grace we need to work together and have the true understanding of our position in ISKCON: we are in fact your ambassadors. We need to show we care rather than just say we care, we need to understand your movement, we need to understand what it is that you want us to do, and over and above all we must be well-wishers of all regardless of color, creed, or status. Please give us the intelligence to spread your message effectively and be valuable members of your society. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is due to your causeless mercy that our good fortune has been created; for this, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are all eternally indebted to your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that all our activities are dovetailed in serving you, and please chastise us when the need arises. Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you very much for creating our good fortune and picking us up from the gutters of material suffering.

Again we thank you for everything that you have done for the conditioned souls in this world.

Your most fallen and insignificant servants in Eastern Cape, South Africa.  
(written by Śyāmlāl dāsa)

## East Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

I imagine that most or all devotees who write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you feel themselves unqualified. Who is qualified to sufficiently glorify you? It certainly is not I, but I will try, out of duty and love.

At the age of seventy you came to a foreign country with practically no money and started a worldwide spiritual movement. You sacrificed all personal comforts to please your spiritual master and Lord Kṛṣṇa. You opened 108 temples, distributed millions of books and unlimited amounts of *prasādam,* and changed the lives of countless fallen souls. You gave us meaning and direction in life and offered hope in an otherwise hopeless situation. You established a worldwide family of devotees, so we can associate with the nice people in the world.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at the ISKCON center in East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.  
(written by Pyāri Mohan dāsa)

## Ekaterinburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories unto you on this auspicious day of your appearance!

If devotees are asked what is the most important day in their lives, almost all of them say that it was the day when they came in touch with the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. That day changed their lives. When you traveled to America in 1965, most of us were little children. It is wonderful that by your mercy we now have the opportunity to study such sacred books as *Bhagavad-gītā, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* here in the Ural Mountains of Russia. Thank you very much for saving us from material existence and sins. We try to follow your instructions, and your sincere disciples help us and inspire us on this path. We always feel your mercy and care.

By your mercy the devotees of New Ekacakra-dhāma here in Ekaterinburg have a center with Deities of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga. Now everyone can come and see the beautiful forms of the Lord and get Their mercy.

*Saṅkīrtana* is the main part of Lord Caitanya’s teachings, which you gave us. Every Sunday we gather together for chanting the holy names of the Lord and discussing scriptures. In summer we go on *harināmas,* where we sing and dance in ecstasy and in this way express our love and joy. *Harināma* has become popular among the citizens of Ekaterinburg. By your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we put on Ratha-yātrā festivals every summer, and the fortunate people of Ekaterinburg may get the *darśana* of Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā.

Reading and discussing scriptures is another part of the *sādhana* you gave us. During Sunday programs we discuss the *Bhagavad-gītā* and other scriptures. Many people are interested to read your books. The devotees distribute books in the city and go on traveling *saṅkīrtana* around the Ural region. Our *yātrā*takes part in the December marathon every year. Thank you for your books!

*Prasādam* distribution is also one of our main services. We distribute *prasādam* on *harināmas* and during Sunday programs, and serve feasts at festivals. In this way even the hearts of the most fallen souls may be purified. Thank you for teaching us the yoga of cooking, offering, honoring, and distributing sacred food!

The Nāma-haṭṭa program is very popular and successful in Ekaterinburg. There are ten Nāma-haṭṭa groups, where the devotees and their guests may meet for devotional service. Many *gṛhasthas* worship Deities of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga at home.

By your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Vedic knowledge and culture are becoming accessible to the people of Russia. Here in Ekaterinburg we may attend *bhakta* programs and *bhakti-śāstrī* classes led by senior devotees. Thank you for saving us from ignorance!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our modest efforts on the path of *bhakti*. We always need your mercy and blessings.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for showing us the way of *bhakti*. Thank you for our dear Lord, who is full of happiness and joy, who is the greatest singer and dancer, who is always beautiful and young, and who is merciful and compassionate to the most fallen.

Your grandsons and granddaughters in New Ekacakra, Ekaterinburg, Russia.

## Gītā-nāgarī Baru

Most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. You have written so many books. You live forever by your instructions, and the followers live with you. The most important is the instruction to chant at least sixteen rounds daily of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. In this you are following Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who instructed, *kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ:* “Always chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.”

This is also mentioned in the *Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa:*

*harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam*  
*kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva nāsty eva gatir anyathā*

**“In this age of quarrel and hypocrisy, the only means of deliverance is chanting the holy name of the Lord. There is no other way, there is no other way, there is no other way.”**

You have instructed us to cooperate as proof of our love for you. It is *māyā* if we fight, and you have said the danger of our fighting among ourselves is much worse than the danger of demons fighting against us. Let us be honest and humble with one another.

You want us to always preach and not be lazy. We will please you by doing some solid work for the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, whether in book distribution, farm development, festivals, *gurukula—*there is, it seems, no end to the varieties of preaching in this movement.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, because of your mercy and the mercy of your disciples, His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami Mahārāja and His Grace Gaura Maṇḍala Bhūmi Prabhu, we established the village of Gītā Nāgarī Baru at Lampung, Indonesia, on March 13th 1999. We have worked to develop agriculture with no chemicals, no electricity, no plastics, and slowly, step by step, no dependence on modern products.

You have taught actual self-sufficiency, which means to depend on what is real: Kṛṣṇa consciousness, Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotees, Kṛṣṇa’s cows, and Kṛṣṇa’s land. So we hope, by your mercy, that we can maintain Kṛṣṇa’s cows nicely and have no problem with the land.

You wanted to establish the *dharma* of four *āśramas* and four *varṇas*. We are trying to help you do so with our Gopāla Kindergarten, founded in August 2003. Now we have twenty-five children. As Prahlāda Mahārāja instructed, it is a must from the beginning for the parents to teach their children about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You also were always very, very enthusiastic that young students get trained in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Therefore, we are trying to train the children in the art of loving Kṛṣṇa. We hope they will know how to serve the order of the spiritual master and will be able to save everyone by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness far and wide. We hope that by your mercy we can preach to them nicely and they will glow always in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

On this occasion, we remember your divine appearance on this planet. Now you have left, but you also remain, as when you came one time to Indonesia in March 1973. You have sent your dear devotees like His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami Mahārāja and His Grace Gaura Maṇḍala Bhūmi Prabhu. They always give us guidance and association so that we can, step by step, understand that the real goal of life is to attain *kṛṣṇa-prema,* or pure devotional service.

*na hy adbhutaṁ tvac-caraṇābja-reṇubhir*  
*hatāṁhaso bhaktir adhokṣaje 'malā*  
*mauhūrtikād yasya samāgamāc ca me*  
*dustarka-mūlo ’pahato ’vivekaḥ*

**“It is not at all wonderful that simply by being covered by the dust of your lotus feet, one immediately attains the platform of pure devotional service to Adhokṣaja, which is not available even to great demigods like Brahmā. By associating with you just for a moment, I am now freed from all argument, false prestige, and lack of discrimination, which are the roots of entanglement in the material world. Now I am free from all these problems.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **5.13.22)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are devotional service personified. We pray to guru and Lord Kṛṣṇa to let us always appreciate your divine nature. We don’t have any strength. We beg that you take us, just as the breeze carries the raindrops to their destination, to the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla and engage us in Their loving devotional service.

*prema-bhakti yāhā haite avidyā vināśa yāte*

It is only because of your presence that we have all remembered that our eternal obligation is to return home, back to Godhead. *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Being completely useless, we beg you that birth after birth you give us the shelter of your wonderful lotus feet. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O bona fide spiritual master, you are *patita-pāvana,* the *jagat-guru,* you are the guide for all our activities, you are the reservoir of all enlightenment. Without your mercy there is no possibility of solving the problems of doubt. Without your direction we cannot take even one step forward. You are free from all material contamination, and you are always meditating upon the Supreme Personality of Godhead. After wandering for countless years in the material universe, we now have the opportunity of association that is uncontaminated by the modes of nature. You are unlimitedly merciful. Please bestow upon us the benediction of liberation from this present dangerous material way of life. You are an unalloyed devotee, and you have no desire other then to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa. You are simply ever engaged in worshiping Him in full surrender and always hearing and chanting about His activities, which are wonderful and auspicious. Thus you are always merged in an ocean of transcendental bliss. We offer our respectful obeisance unto you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, who lead the world *saṅkīrtana* party known as ISKCON, please continue to manifest yourself to those who remain in your movement and listen to your song. You appeared like a lightning flash. You have come from the spiritual world. You have changed our lives, and we are as if touched by the wand of your spiritual form. You make us so fond of you. You are a perfect person. Your personal example has given us new hope. We thank you again and again. We fall at your lotus feet. You are certainly protecting us always by imparting wise instructions.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our beloved spiritual master, please give us the ability to speak the philosophy nicely and warmly, in a realized way, with honor and concern for all living entities, and please help us be satisfied with just the holy name and give up any desire to enjoy Lord Kṛṣṇa’s energies. Please help us control the mind and senses, and also please grant us the humility to understand that we are your servants.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this day of your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, please give us the strength to deserve this great fortune and to fulfill your desire that we be always engaged in devotional service. Hare Kṛṣṇa. *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your humble servants in Gītā Nāgarī Baru, Lampung, Indonesia.  
(written by Ṣaḍbhuja Gaurāṅga dāsa)

## Gothenburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the divine dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you and your sincere followers!

Your divine mercy showers the world. It is amazing to see how the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is growing despite all difficulties and tribulations. This is a time when people are getting increasingly confused about their identities and relationship to the Supreme. Your pioneer effort to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness has taken root here in the West, and is benefiting thousands of people.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, came to the West like a lion. You presented Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a disarming and straightforward way. You did not compromise or water down the eternal essence of *sanātana-dharma*. You did not compromise to accommodate local customs. Your desire is to please your Guru Mahārāja and the Supreme Lord, and your purity wins more and more hearts.

You are the most perfect preacher of *bhakti,* the eternal loving relationship of servitorship to the Lord. You have established the eternal difference between the servant and the Lord. This difference is not given in a world that tends towards *nirviśeṣa,* nondifference, and where servitorship to the Lord is the exception rather then the norm. As the founder-*ācārya,* you gave us the example and the path to establish that relationship.

Your loyalty and dedication to your spiritual master never cease to astound us. Your true greatness shines forth in your deep commitment to your guru. By your example you have shown us how to satisfy the spiritual master. Even in old age you sacrificed your own comfort and ventured outside India to save the suffering people of the world.

We are very grateful and eternally indebted to Your Divine Grace for showing us the way out of this material world, back to the Lord’s lotus feet.

We humbly beg for your blessings. Please let us always be engaged in your service, and in the service of your sincere followers.

Your humble servants in Gothenburg, Sweden.

## Greece

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We offer our most humble obeisances at the dust of the lotus feet of our eternal spiritual master and father, His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please have mercy on us. Because we are so fallen, that is our only hope. Only by your divine mercy can we become qualified to serve you, our spiritual masters, and the Vaiṣṇava devotees of the Lord.

*yadyapi āmāra guru—caitanyera dasa*  
*tathāpi jāniye āmi tāṅhāra prakāśa*

**“Although I know that my spiritual master is a servitor of Śrī Caitanya, I know Him also as a plenary manifestation of the Lord.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.44)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your divine teachings and by showing us your personal example, you captivated our minds and hearts forever.

*ācinoti yaḥśāstrārtham ācāre sthāpayanty api*  
*svayam ācarate yasmād ācāryas tena kīrtitaḥ*

**“An** ***ācārya*** **is one who fully understands the conclusions of the revealed scriptures and whose behavior reflects his deep realization. He is a living example, for he teaches the meaning of the scriptures by both word and deed.” (*****Vāyu Purāṇa*****)**

So many years and lifetimes we have simply wasted our time by performing only useless activities, and now in this human form of life only by your causeless mercy, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, have we come back to our senses from the madness of material existence. By our rendering service to our material mind and senses for so many years—to society, friends, ideas, fantasies—the only result we got was pain and disappointment. By trying to enjoy our youthful lives we received happiness equal in value to a drop of water in the desert. But by your special mercy, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your deep compassion, we now understand that real happiness is to be found in the association of devotees and in rendering service to them and to the Supreme Couple, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we bow down before you. Please, O master, have mercy upon us and accept us in your family. You are our eternal spiritual master, our worshipable guru, our father, our everything. You are our life and soul.

Your servants in Greece.

## Guyana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Vyāsa-pūjā homages fulfill several functions in addition to glorifying the guru, a most significant one of which, we feel, is to renew our allegiance to your mission and instructions. By giving prominence to various attributes of yours and pointing out the goals we can achieve by following your orders, we hope to draw attention to your great legacy, which we must attempt to preserve.

As always, we are extremely happy to offer Your Divine Grace our prayers on this auspicious day of your appearance. Year after year we try to present you with a progress report of our *yātrā* as a sign of our dedication to you. Over the past five years we embarked on the construction of several new temples. During the past year we also decided to train ourselves and our members in diverse aspects of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Every year we plan to send at least two men to attend Vaiṣṇava training courses in India. We plan to pursue this vigorously in the upcoming years, as well as running courses locally. We are convinced that refining our devotee resources is a most valuable investment.

This year, also, we started to reemphasize centering our *yātrā*on your books. First, we are spending more time reading your books, and second, we are stressing that our most important duty is to push book distribution. We are confident that, as a result of this, all auspiciousness will prevail and simultaneously all our financial needs will be met in the Guyana *yātrā*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you maintain our *bhakti*. We are feeble in the process of devotional service and cannot even claim to be your direct servants, but we are satisfied being servants of your servants. We know that if we sincerely serve your mission our adulterated consciousness will disappear and we will feel greater and greater pleasure in hearing about, chanting about, and remembering Kṛṣṇa. And by *seva* to your pure devotees, all obstacles will vanish.

We met you through your books and your representatives, and in this way you completely transformed our lives. Somehow we are trying to serve you and spread this most magnificent *saṅkīrtana* movement. Today we continue to beg for your blessings, because without them our individual attempts would never bear fruit. Please help us become mature and useful servants of your wonderful society, ISKCON.

Your insignificant spiritual grandchildren at ISKCON’s centers in Guyana, South America.  
(written by Paramātmā dāsa)

## Helsinki

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our repeated humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious 108th anniversary of your appearance, we, the devotees of the Helsinki temple, would like to express our gratitude for the following gifts you have given us and the world:

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us; (1) the all-powerful, all-auspicious, all-victorious and all-blissful *mahā-mantra;* (2) the all-successful, all-blissful, and all-perfect *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam;* (3) the all-glorious *Bhagavad-gītā;* (4) the all-nectarean *Caitanya-caritāmṛta;* (5) the all-devotional *Nectar of Devotion; (*6) the all-sublime *Kṛṣṇa* book; (7) all the other spiritual literatures with their purports, your emotional ecstasies; (8) the association of Your Divine Grace through these books and through; (9) your lectures; (10) your letters.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for; (11) connecting us to *śrī-guru-paramparā; (*12) the opportunity to chant Gāyatrī mantras; (13) *harināma-saṅkīrtana;* (14) the process of *bhakti; (*15) the Deities of the Supreme Lord; (16) Śrīmatī Tulasī-devī; (17) *ceto-darpaṇa-mārjanam,* the way to be purified of the six vices.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for revealing to us; (18) Śrī Māyāpur-dhāma; (19) Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma; (20) Śrī Jagannātha Purī-dhāma; (21) mother Gaṅgā; (22) mother Yamunā; (23) the Janmāṣṭamī festival; (24) the Gaura Pūrṇimā festival; (25) the Navadvīpa-maṇḍala *parikramā;* (26) the Vraja-maṇḍala *parikramā;* (27) Śrī Girirāja.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for teaching us; (28) Vaiṣṇava culture; (29) *yukta-vairāgya; (*30) discrimination between matter and spirit; (31) the proverbs of Cāṇakya Paṇḍita; (32) the Sanskrit language; (33) the Bengali language; (34) knowledge of chanting *ślokas;* (35) the vision of learned men; (36) real morality; (37) Vedic cosmology; (38) the Vaiṣṇava educational system; (39) Vaiṣṇava *sevā;* (40) *atithi-sevā;* (41) Tulasī*sevā;* (42) appreciation of our own mother; (43) appreciation of mother cow; (44) appreciation of mother earth; (45) the *brahmacārī-āśrama;* (46) the *gṛhastha-āśrama;* (47) the *vānaprastha-āśrama;* (48) the *sannyāsa-āśrama;* (49) the different *varṇas;* (50) celibacy; (51) playing Vaiṣṇava instruments; (52) singing *bhajans* and *kīrtanas;* (53) control of the mind and senses; (54) the value of human life; (55) the value of time; (56) Āyurveda.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us (57) *Kṛṣṇa-*book reading sessions; (58) blissful *bhāgavata-prasādam,* especially cheese *samosās.*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for defeating (59) the mundane rascal scientists; (60) other atheists; (61) the Māyāvādīs; (62) the *nirviśeṣavādīs;* (63) the *śūnyavādīs;* (64) the *apasampradāyas;* (65) the pseudo yogis; (66) the bogus *avatāras;* (67) the monkey renunciants; (68) the pseudo religious sentimentalists; (69) the Western so-called philosophers; (70) the demigod worshipers; (71) the mundane religionists; (72) the communists; (73) the capitalists; (74) other fruitive workers; (75) the sense gratifiers in general; (76) the dry renunciants.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us the opportunity to serve you and Śrī Pañca-tattva through (77) book distribution; (78) *prasādam* distribution; (79) Deity worship; (80) college preaching; (81) public programs; (82) *harināmas;* (83) Radio Kṛṣṇa; (84) Food for Life; (85) direct preaching; (86) indirect preaching; (87) Nāma-haṭṭa preaching; (88) making life members; (89) making the Māyāpur *adbhuta-mandira;* (90) *gurukulas;* (91) your wonderful disciples.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupada, thank you for giving us (92) the protection of Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva’s nails; (93) the shelter Śrī Śrī Gāndharvikā-Giridhārīs lotus feet; (94) the mercy of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for being (95) an ocean of good qualities; (96) learned; (97) strict; (98) devotional; (99) surrendered; (100) ingenious; (101) humorous; (102) peaceful; (103) blissful; (104) tolerant; (105) compassionate.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for (106) coming to the Western countries; (107) writing the *Jaladuta* prayers; (108) connecting us to Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva’s pastimes.

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Helsinki, Finland.  
(written by Munirāja dāsa)

## Houston

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Hare Kṛṣṇa Dhām in Houston is truly an international temple, with devotees from all over the world. Right now we are mostly of Indian descent, but our intimate congregation is from all over, including China, Guyana, Holland, Great Britain, Fiji, the United States, and other places. You created an international society for people of different races and backgrounds to understand their profound relationship with Kṛṣṇa. Presently our rather large temple is a bit too small to fit all the people attracted by the nectar of your association—through your books and the *kīrtanas* you have so nicely taught us. Presently from 500 to 800 people attend the Sunday program, and many more come on festival days.

Overcrowding in the temple is the main reason to expand and to include a restaurant. We have already built a facility named Gaurāṅga Hall, which has a large kitchen, an extra-large lecture hall with a stage, classrooms, and offices; it is roughly 14,000 square feet. The community has rented the hall out for wedding receptions, marriage anniversaries, and graduation parties, which the temple caters and which bring many new people to Hare Kṛṣṇa Dhām. The quarter-acre grounds are beautifully landscaped, with benches, trees, flowers, and grass. Your servant Tamāl Krishna Goswami planned everything to please you. Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for coming to the West at such an advanced age and giving us the greatest of gifts—*bhakti*. We are hoping to serve you in some small manner.

*Your servants in Houston, Texas, USA.*  
(written by Pāṇḍava-vijaya dāsa)

## Karuna Bhavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most important day, your Vyāsa-pūjā. All glories to your ISKCON and to your transcendental literatures, which are the saviors of all the fallen souls.

Every year as it comes time to glorify Your Divine Grace on Vyāsa-pūjā day, the thing that becomes most prominent is just how important Your Divine Grace is to all the conditioned souls living on this planet. By your transcendental activities and dedication to the mission of your spiritual master, you have given the world “the matchless gift.” Kṛṣṇa consciousness is indeed the most precious benediction that the living entities can hope for or acquire, and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, made this readily available in all corners of the globe. Your teachings are perfect in every respect, and one who reads your books in a mood of sincere inquiry will find answers to all the confusing issues that make up material life. You have taken great time and chosen every word with full deliberation so that the message of the transcendental world and the means to attain that world are perfectly presented.

One special quality of the literary works of Your Divine Grace is how many persons from less-than-good backgrounds change as they meet your instructions and take to practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness. They become changed from low-class to actual high-class as they take these teachings and principles to heart. This is indeed the sign of a great personality. Previously there were many wonderful *sādhus* and saintly devotees, but who can claim to have had the same impact upon the world at large as Your Divine Grace?

Many persons become surprised when they hear of how large your society is, but you were always convinced that Kṛṣṇa consciousness would spread throughout the entire world. That has come to be, and your society continues to expand through the inspiration given by you to your disciples and from those disciples to their own disciples and congregations. Your movement is most powerful because it is driven by the Absolute Truth. Of course, in the light of Absolute Truth there will be many disturbances and attempts to minimize and discredit such a presentation, but in the long term one cannot deny or suppress such truths. You have left us such a great legacy, and there is never any shortage or unemployment in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If we give ourselves fully to your mission of preaching and spreading the message of Lord Caitanya, then life will be very blissful and successful. Then Kṛṣṇa consciousness will be not just a philosophy but our very existence. This year we hope to continue distributing your books, and we hope that you will be pleased by our endeavors. All we can do is live by the principles you gave us: preaching is the essence and books are the basis. Without that which is essential and that which forms the base of your society, we can never be strong nor indeed do that which is of the utmost importance.

Now in Scotland many people are chanting the holy names of the Lord, and it is wonderful to see how they accept these names and the devotees. It can be said that much has changed since the early days of preaching and that the Lord’s name is gradually entering the homes and hearts of the populace. At some point this year we will start to distribute sets of your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatams,* which we hope pleases you very much. Vyāsa-pūjā means not only glorifying the spiritual master but also making a positive offering of service at his lotus feet, and that we intend to do by distributing your transcendental books. Having so many books to distribute is a wonderful opulence, and all of this has been made possible by the hard work of Your Divine Grace. Please continue to bless us with your mercy, inspiration, and transcendental favor.

All glories to Your Divine Grace! Please forgive us for our many faults. We hope and pray that they will become corrected in your service.

Your insignificant servants in Scotland.

## Katikati

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, another year has gone by, yet who you are and what you achieved is no less astounding now than it was when we first heard of you. In fact, as time passes, we pray that we can more fully comprehend your greatness.

From the very beginning you had those remarkable qualities that paved the path of your incredible success. May we forever remember your true life story.

In your childhood, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda were your reality. You grew up hearing and seeing your father’s worship. Child’s play, Ratha-yātrā? No way. You were Kṛṣṇa conscious from the very start. As a growing boy you were already an influential leader whom your friends would look up to. And as a young man you were certainly revolutionary, rejecting your diploma for a higher cause.

Then came that fateful day when you first met your eternal spiritual master. How wonderful it was! The first words he spoke to you were to be your life’s meditation. Tough married years followed, with many struggles—the business fluctuating and the family “breaking the axe on your head.” Again your spiritual master marked you and reinforced your mission: “He will do everything when the time comes” and “If you ever get money, print books.” Then your spiritual master disappeared and the Gauḍīya Maṭha crumbled, but your spiritual objectives pressed more and more. You inaugurated *Back to Godhead* magazine despite your personal hardships. *Yasyāham anugṛhṇāmi:* You saw the Lord’s hand throughout, showing determination and dedication regardless of seemingly endless setbacks.

When you started the League of Devotees, your devotion and purity were clear to see. Few takers, though, but your deep wisdom pervaded: “India was following the West, so make the Western world Kṛṣṇa conscious, and then India would follow.” Home life ended with “Tea or me!” Biscuits for the *Bhāgavatam,* wife’s last stand.

*Back to Godhead* was being published regularly despite all odds. Immersed in the effort—writing and printing and selling—but what else was there? You simply had no other desire.

“Take *sannyāsa,*” your Guru Mahārāja urged you for the third time in a dream. And so you did. Again you heard your Guru speak through others: “Write books, they’re permanent.” Miraculous it was that you were able to print those three volumes! Incredible endeavor, endless pushing, there was no question of defeat.

Oh! What it took to get on that *Jaladuta*! You were unstoppable. You had a vision; you practically knew it was all going to happen—“I have temples and followers; just time is separating.” Lord Caitanya predicted it, so it was to be. But you get the credit, Śrīla Prabhupāda. It was your example that changed us. Free from any material motive, you simply cared with unparalleled compassion. You were all-attractive, the living proof of every word you spoke. Your complete purity was so apparent.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were and still are the life breath of ISKCON.

May we derive strength from your endless supply of strength. May we develop even an iota of determination and vision such as yours, and may you become the most famous person this world has ever seen. Please bestow your blessings upon us.

Your servants in Western Bay of Plenty (the Katikati-Tauranga yātrā), New Zealand.  
(written by Rāmacandra dāsa)

## Kecskemet

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances. All glories to you!

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our spiritual father and the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON! Only your grace has enabled the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement to grow worldwide. All glories to you! And all glories to your disciples, who guide and teach us as spiritual masters. A few years ago we had practically nothing in Kecskemet. And now we have a nice temple, almost a dozen enthusiastic devotees, and a congregation of sixty to seventy. In this temple we have enthusiastic preaching: Our *saṅkīrtana* devotees, those who distribute books, are Hungary’s top distributors. Now we have started a Food for Life program and organized various courses. For all this we thank Your Divine Grace, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda. A mere decade ago we didn’t even know who Kṛṣṇa was, but now we worship Him and teach others how to worship Him. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the process of devotional service you brought to Europe works perfectly.

Thank you for all these blessings. We beg Your Divine Grace to enable us to serve you and lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission eternally.

Your devotees in Kecskemet, Hungary.

## Kuala Lumpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Jaya Nitya-līlā-praviṣṭa Oṁ Viṣṇu-pāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A. C. Bhaktivedānta Svāmī Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

All glories to your divine spiritual father, our great-grandfather guru, the *siṁha-guru* Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, the personified energy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy!

Jaya Nitya-līlā-praviṣṭa Oṁ Viṣṇu-pāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

All glories to Śrī Śrī Gauḍīyanātha and Śrī Śrī Gopīnātha!

On this most auspicious day we offer our repeated prostrated obeisances unto the dust of your lotus feet, a dust which can deliver millions of illusioned souls, thus astounding even Lord Brahmā, the original progenitor.

Today the three worlds celebrate this most glorious anniversary of the descent of the Lord’s true emissary, who was destined to carry out the mission of Śrīmān Mahāprabhu, to fulfill His heartfelt desire that His holy name would be chanted in every town and village. You are the *senapati-bhakta* whom Lord Caitanya predicted would appear, as recorded by Locana dāsa Ṭhākura in his *Caitanya-maṅgala*. You are the *ācārya* who built a house for the whole world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, glory unto thee, glory unto thee!

All the oceans of superlative qualities have merged into the Bhaktivedanta *sindhu,* thus inundating the world with *kalyāna guṇas*—mercy, compassion, humility, forbearance, magnanimity.

By dint of your immense power and purity, Your Divine Grace has invited Their Lordships to descend. Who could fathom this?

The advent of the Pañca-tattva certifies your astute conviction that this movement is empowered by Śrīmān Mahāprabhu. We are a branch of the Mahāprabhu tree, as you have said.

Hear ye, hear ye! Let the skeptics and the envious hold their tongues, for the Lord has manifested in His *arcā-vigraha* form to fulfill the prophecy and desire of His pure servant.

“No matter what,” you said, “never leave this movement.” This boat of ISKCON that Your Divine Grace has built shall ferry us across the ocean of nescience. And now, more than ever, our faith in those sublime words of yours has crystallized within the core of our hearts.

The temple that Your Divine Grace is building—now known as the Śrī Māyāpur Candrodaya Mandir and in the future as The Temple of the Vedic Planetarium—is destined to become the epicenter of the *saṅkīrtana* renaissance of the world.

Your Divine Grace’s vision is unfolding right before our eyes. And you are very much with us at every step. He reasons ill who says you have left us. You live forever within the core of the hearts of your ardent followers. *Vāṇī-seva—*following the instructions you gave in your books, letters, lectures, and conversations—and *vapu-sevā*serving the cause of ISKCON, your body—are the sole pleasures of our desolate and arid lives.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, but still we miss you. Placing you on the altar of our hearts, we shall worship you. And the day we forget to glorify you shall be the darkest day in our lives.

At Śrī Jagannātha Mandir your insignificant servants are working to fulfill your mission. We pray to become worthy servants of Your Divine Grace.

Though lame and ill-advised, full of faults and ignorant, we shall try. Try and cry. We shall endeavor to work together on the platform of love and trust, learn to be humble and tolerant, to be serious and steadfast in our *sādhana,* to read and distribute your books, give our lives to serve the Vaiṣṇavas, become *dāsa-dāsa-anudāsa*.

Let this be our solemn vow to Your Divine Grace on this day. We pray that we daily meditate on this prayer.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, what have we got to offer you but our lives?

What have we got to give but our love?

What is our one desire? To be a speck of dust at your *caraṇāravinda*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength and resilience, the courage and determination, to offer our bodies, minds, and words on the sacrificial altar of the *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. Only then will our lives will be fruitful.

Patita-pāvana Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī jaya!

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia.  
(written by Uttama Caitanya dāsa)

## KwaZulu Natal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha and Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu!

By your mercy . . .

. . . we were able to witness how one of your very dear servants, Srutakīrti Prabhu, ultimately surrendered at your lotus feet. His saintly departure from this world while holding tightly onto your mercy inspires us to surrender more unto your lotus feet, as those two lotus feet are the only abode of shelter and protection in this world of danger and distress.

By your mercy . . .

. . . we were able to once again experience the happiness the *saṅkīrtana* mission offers through distributing your books. One of the biggest book marathons held in many years has revealed to us that all success will come and that everything will be taken care of if we simply focus on book distribution.

By your mercy . . .

. . . arguably the largest Ratha-yātrā festival outside India was celebrated recently in Durban. Hundreds of thousands of visitors were blessed with Lord Jagannātha’s mercy via the various forms of preaching that were presented—dramas, temple worship, *prasādam* distribution, ecstatic and soothing chanting of the holy names, massive book distribution, and two colorful and blissful processions, with Your Divine Grace and Śrī Jagannātha, Śrī Baladeva, and Subhadrā Mahārāṇī compassionately glancing out on everyone.

By your mercy . . .

. . . the temple project in Phoenix is scheduled to be completed this year. The temple is already largely successful, with many hundreds attending programs weekly in the incomplete hall. Devotees are eagerly awaiting the descent of the forms of the Lord with which you desire to bless this *yātrā*.

By your mercy . . .

. . . many projects, small and large, like the increasing Food for Life programs developing in many areas in KwaZulu Natal, including Stanger and Tongaat, are steadily growing; renovations to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha’s home are being planned and executed. To please you, so many devotees have put aside all personal difficulties and are assuming responsibility for the various needs of this *yātrā*. Simply to please you.

So much can be achieved by your mercy. Simply by your desire and the devotees’ aspiration to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, according to your desire, Śrī Pañca-tattva has gloriously descended in Śrīdhām Māyāpur. If we choose to sincerely serve Your Divine Grace, KwaZulu Natal will also be engulfed in the waves of transcendental ecstasy resounding from Māyāpur.

Lord Caitanya will do anything for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. The flow of His mercy is slowed or impeded only by our unwillingness to serve you, or our laziness and apathy. When we choose to selflessly serve Your Divine Grace, all success is inevitably present, no matter what the external results may be. And your mercy is that you have lovingly allowed us to serve Your Divine Grace.

May we eternally engage in just serving you.

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa.

## Lagos

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you!

Out of great compassion for all living entities, you brought the most valuable and rare treasure from Goloka and spread it all over the world, even to the “dark continent” of Africa. So now we have the chance to develop our dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness by your causeless mercy.

By founding the International Society for Krishna Consciousness (ISKCON), you have given everyone a home and also a chance to become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

We are struggling, trying to follow the process; therefore we pray that you kindly bless us so that we can spread the process of chanting the holy name to every town and village. Thank you very much for such wonderful mercy.

Your eternally indebted devotees in the ISKCON temple in Lagos, Nigeria.

## Las Vegas

My dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

You are the savior of my life, and you are the guide and protector of my tiny creeper of devotional life in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. All glories to you!

Unfortunately, during my journey on the path of going back to Kṛṣṇa, I lost my spiritual father and guide a few years ago, but I still feel safe at the shelter of your lotus feet. Your ISKCON institution is my house, your books are my guide in spiritual education, your GBC representatives are my spiritual authorities, and your dedicated followers are my *śikṣā* gurus. Your mission of spreading Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s glories is the purpose of my life, and your satisfaction is my only goal. Please let me perform only the activities that bring you satisfaction. Please give me the intelligence to execute my duties in a way pleasing to you. Please empower me to carry out your orders with great enthusiasm and determination.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are my source of inspiration. The more I learn about your unbelievable achievements in spreading Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s glories all over the world, your unlimited love for the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, your unalloyed devotion to your Guru Mahārāja, and your strong faith in his words and mission, which you pushed forward with an extraordinary enthusiasm and determination among the degraded people of this ignorant age, the more I am convinced of your superhuman powers, your divine powers.

Just as Rūpa and Sanātana Gosvāmīs, Rāmānanda Rāya, and other pure devotees of the Lord confirmed the real identity of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu from the Vedic scriptures, so your strict and dedicated followers, whom you empowered in the spiritual science, have confirmed from the same Vedic scriptures that you are the highest *ācārya,* the most exalted devotee of the Lord, and the greatest *paramahaṁsa,* with a unique spiritual power given by God Himself.

Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa has reciprocated your loving affection toward Him, and therefore you were able, by His Mercy, to accomplish miraculous things possible for no ordinary living entity.

In the purport to *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya* 7.11–12, you write:

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura explains that unless one is directly empowered by the causeless mercy of Kṛṣṇa, one cannot become the spiritual master of the entire world (*jagad-guru*). . . . \[T\]he spiritual master of the entire world must be considered an incarnation of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy.

Also, Cc *Antya-līlā* 2.13–14 states:

To deliver people in regions throughout the universe who could not meet Him, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu personally entered the bodies of pure devotees. Thus He empowered living beings \[His pure devotees\] by manifesting in them so much of His own devotion that people in all other countries became devotees by seeing them.

And in your divine purport to this verse you wrote:

Unless one is empowered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, one cannot spread the holy names of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* throughout the world. Persons who do so are empowered. Therefore they are sometimes called *āveśa-avatāras,* or empowered incarnations, for they are endowed with the power of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

In addition, in *Teachings of Lord Caitanya* you write:

Examples of indirect *avatāras* are the four Kumāras, Nārada, Pṛthu and Paraśurāma. These are actually living entities, but there is specific power given to them by the Supreme Personality of Godhead. When a specific opulence of the Supreme Lord is invested in specific entities, they are called *āveśa-avatāras*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you had this power conferred upon you to such a degree that you are the world *ācārya* and therefore an *āveśa-avatāra*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have introduced Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere; therefore you are Jagat Guru.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *nitya-siddha,* eternally perfect.

In a letter to Tamāl Krishna Mahārāja in 1970 you wrote:

Although I had immense opportunities to indulge in the four principles of sinful life because I was connected with a very aristocratic family, Krsna always saved me, and throughout my whole life I do not know what is illicit sex, intoxication, meat-eating or gambling. So far my present life is concerned, I do not remember any part of my life when I was forgetful of Krsna.

Personally, I don’t know the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. I know nothing about the spiritual world, and I have no realization of my spiritual identity. I don’t understand what is love of God or how to perform devotional service for His satisfaction. But although I do not know what real love is, I perfectly know lust, greed, anger, and all forms of gross and subtle sense gratification. Being in this material world since time immemorial, I have lost the memory of my spiritual identity; therefore I am completely acquainted only with the bodily conception of life and am full of bad habits.

However, you are known as *patita-pāvana,* the savior of the most fallen souls in this age of dense darkness, where the degradation increases day by day. I am the unfortunate victim of this Kali Age, but by your unlimited compassion and causeless mercy toward such conditioned fallen souls as me, you have mercifully offered me shelter under your ISKCON roof. This is another of your unmatched glories. It is said that usually a son develops the qualities of his father, and thus you have inherited all the good qualities of your Supreme Father, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. He describes Himself in *Bhagavad-gītā* 9.29: “I envy no one, nor am I partial to anyone. I am equal to all. But whoever renders service unto Me in devotion is a friend, is in Me, and I am also a friend to him.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you posses exactly the same characteristics as your Supreme Father, and a simple proof of it is that you have mercifully taken me under your loving care. When Lord Nityānanda Prabhu wanted to enlighten Jagāi and Mādhāi, He thought:

If I can convince them to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and become Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s devotees, then My beloved Lord will become even more famous because of such miscreants and rascals surrendering to Him. In this way, everybody will think about the Supreme Lord’s greatness—that He can make possible out of impossible.

Similarly, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you acted like Lord Nityānanda Prabhu. By liberating such a conditioned soul as myself, who resembled Jagāi and Mādhāi in behavior, you brought more glory to the Supreme Lord by showing that even such a fallen soul as my insignificant self was able to become convinced by your teachings to surrender to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

By your mercy I see a lot of positive changes in my life. And by your mercy I can understand that actually this mercy alone enables me to make spiritual progress. Observing those things that have miraculously changed for the better in my life and the lives of others as well, my heart is full of hope that by remaining at the shelter of your lotus feet and following your instructions I will make further spiritual progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Even if the full purification will take me many lifetimes, still it is wonderful that by your causeless mercy I am on the path of going home, going back to Godhead, to the spiritual world. Without your inconceivable mercy, nothing will happen, however. Therefore I beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help me continue rendering devotional service and help me accept the full shelter of the divine dust of your lotus feet.

Badrinārāyaṇ Prabhu, your GBC representative and my present direct authority, sent me a letter in which wrote:

Śrīla Prabhupāda said that the grandfather is even kinder and more affectionate to the grandchildren than the father. Both of you are on the frontlines \[he also refers to my wife\], making great sacrifices to serve Śrīla Prabhupāda. There is no doubt that he is pleased and is blessing you. So you also have an intimate relationship with Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Since Badrinārāyaṇ Prabhu represents you, I feel honored by his words of recognition. I understand that it is only because we are sincerely trying to follow your instructions and push forward your mission of spreading the glories of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the most sinful place on this planet, Las Vegas, that my wife—Kṛṣṇa-mayī-devī dāsī, your spiritual granddaughter—and I have an intimate relationship with you.

I am so grateful to you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, that my humble aspiration is to contribute to fulfilling your desires. Recently I have been meditating constantly on how I could play a helpful role in implementing the second part of your mission—establishing *varṇāśrama* in our ISKCON society.

During my discussion on this subject with His Holiness Devāmṛta Mahārāja the last time he visited our temple/home, he encouraged me to contact devotees who are willing to create the opportunities for congregational members, so they can get together and dedicate their time and energy to the projects for developing the economic structure in our ISKCON society, for your pleasure.

GBCs and many temple devotees already perform a wonderful service by supervising and increasing the spiritual standards in ISKCON temples, as you desired. I appreciate this service so much, and I am very grateful to all of them for satisfying you in this very nice and significant way. Observing their endeavor and the effects of their service, I feel peaceful and assured that everything is progressing smoothly there, and you are pleased so much.

However, at the same time I wonder constantly about the thousands and thousands of devotees who live outside the temples. In many cases those devotees, due to their struggle for survival and maintenance, are forced to associate with *karmīs* and sacrifice their valuable time and energy working in *karmī* jobs. Such time and energy could be used in direct devotional service for Kṛṣṇa, but instead they unwillingly support the materialistic economic system and don’t have much time and energy to participate in our ISKCON activities or give more support to our ISKCON projects. I feel this is a complex situation and requires more attention and concern.

My mind is full of different thoughts on this subject. (I described some of these ideas in a large article appearing in the Fall 2003 issue of the *Hare Kṛṣṇa World* newspaper, where our preaching activities in Las Vegas were described for your pleasure).

It is regrettable that many religious movements, which are not bona fide or lack the first-class culture and philosophy you gave us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have organized a social system and economic structure very nicely. For instance, Christians and Muslims have their own hospitals, schools, social and business institutions, and so on.

I read His Holiness Rādhānātha Swami’s conversation after a lecture he gave in March of 1993 in Chowpatty, Bombay. His thoughts completely match my personal feeling on this subject.

I remember one time myself and Paramahaṁsa Kṛṣṇa Swami and a few others, we went on an interfaith tour. And we went to one yoga community, it’s a beautiful community, and these people are so incredibly well organized, it’s unbelievable. They have a construction business and so many of the *gṛhasthas* living there are part of that construction business, and they build houses for free for the community. And they build outside and they make a lot of money and they are maintaining everything. The *sannyāsīs* and the *brahmacārīs* they just need not have to worry about one penny ever. And everything is so nicely organized and so harmonious and everything is just the way you want it to be. And we were just traveling around this community and we were really impressed, thinking, ‘My God, they are so very well organized, we can learn so much from them.’ And everything was so clean and so nice and everyone was just saying ‘Yes, this is such a wonderful place.” . . . And we were thinking, ‘Yes, it’s a wonderful place.’ They worship Lord Śiva there, they have big Nāṭarāj *mūrti* there, he is dancing. So we were thinking so many things we can learn from them, how to organize, how to do business, how to keep everything very peaceful.

It will be very nice if we, your ISKCON devotees, would be able to create such wonderful facilities for our society and people in general for their education in the spiritual knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We can find a wonderful description of such facilities created by Parīkṣit Mahārāja or Yudhiṣṭhira Mahārāja in their kingdoms, for uplifting the consciousness of their subjects. You always stressed the point that Lord Kṛṣṇa is the first-class supreme king, and therefore our duty is to supply first-class service to His lotus feet. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the first-class representative of this first-class supreme king, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Therefore we, as your followers, are obligated to render first-class service for Kṛṣṇa’s satisfaction, under your first-class guidance. By your and Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s empowerment and satisfaction, we should be able to accomplish even more than all those people at the yoga community achieved.

Only in this way is there a possibility of creating peace, happiness, prosperity, and satisfaction in the human society, just as Lord Rāmacandra did during His rule. The omnipotent Supreme Lord showed us an astounding example of a perfect society in India during His pastimes as Lord Rāma, but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, empowered by Him, came personally to establish the kingdom with God at the center all over the world by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

The problem of the modern world is that people want Rāma-rājya, but without God in the center. You, however, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by the strength of your pure love of Godhead, have introduced spiritual knowledge in this godless society. Without God in human society, there is no question of a peaceful, happy, and prosperous life. There is simply misery, suffering, and boredom.

Thus to help people in general you have established devotional service to the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa on every continent of this earth. You were successful because the Supreme Lord loves you so much that He has empowered you to accomplish even more in the history of this planet than He Himself accomplished when He was present here. In this way, the unlimited greatness of the Supreme Lord—who is so interested in displaying the greatness of His beloved devotees like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda—has been displayed. Simultaneously, your success reveals how dear you are to the sweet heart of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and how much love He has for you in reciprocation of your affection.

As you mention in your teachings, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa loves His unalloyed devotees so much that He is always thinking of how to show their greatness, and devotees are always thinking of how to show the greatness of the Lord. Thus Lord Kṛṣṇa, although He could annihilate Dhṛtarāṣṭra’s army on the battlefield of Kurukṣetra in no time, let the Pāṇḍavas do it, and He established them as the saintly rulers of the world. Similarly, Lord Kṛṣṇa sent you to the Western countries to conquer the Māyāvādīs, with their impersonal and voidist philosophies. You accomplished this task vigorously for Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s pleasure and for the satisfaction of your Guru Mahārāja.

Simultaneously, you have also accomplished so many other goals, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as His Holiness Satsvarūpa Goswami Mahārāja wrote in one of his books about you:

Now we are engaged in writing Śrīla Prabhupāda’s biography, and it is very difficult because he has done so much! Such a difficult task of researching his extensive traveling and preaching, his thousands of recorded lectures and conversations, thousands of letters and writings; interviewing the people who knew or met him (religious, business, scientific, political, and educational leaders, the press, his disciples, the common man); trying to fathom his greatness, his devotion to his Guru Mahārāja, his zeal in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, his divine qualities like tolerance and compassion, his physical features; understanding his mission, his goals, his vision; and finally portraying him to the world in the pages of his biography—this can only be done by Śrīla Prabhupāda’s causeless mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I feel very insignificant, fallen, and unworthy of your blessings. However, by your mercy, my wife and I have accepted for your pleasure this challenge of spreading the Lord’s glories in the sinful city of Las Vegas. Las Vegas is known as the capital of Māyās kingdom; therefore it is also called Sin City. Many people wonder how it is possible to remain Kṛṣṇa conscious here, what to speak of spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. However, by dint of your inconceivable mercy everything is possible, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and therefore we have been able to survive here for almost three years. We have taken shelter of your lotus feet, which are the best and safest objects of worship. Therefore, despite the danger of this place, we feel protected and empowered by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

My wife and I foster a burning desire to be more active in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in this Sin City. We are determined in our new mission despite the financial struggles and hardships we face every day, even to pay our monthly bills and mortgage. One of our dreams is to establish a Vedic Cultural Center in Las Vegas, along with a Govinda’s Restaurant.

In addition to these projects, we have a few more ambitious ideas to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Las Vegas. Kṛṣṇa-mayī wants to teach vegetarian cooking classes at a local community college. She hopes to popularize vegetarianism and also give presentations on the subject. The eventual goal is to establish a Vegetarian Society of Las Vegas.

Kṛṣṇa-mayī says,

We desire and we pray every day to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa for the ability to spread His glories all over Las Vegas. Of course, we aim to start fund-raising for bigger projects and somehow raise enough funds to establish a large, beautiful cultural center on the famous Las Vegas Strip, where millions of tourists visit each year from all over the world. It would be so wonderful for them to visit our Hare Kṛṣṇa center and then, upon their return to their hometown, tell others what they have seen and heard in the heart of Sin City. In this way the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness will spread more dynamically, even from this one small place.

Our idea is that this cultural center would include a magnificent Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa temple, a large preaching center, a Govinda’s Restaurant, a *varṇāśrama* college, a *haṭha-yoga* club, a martial arts club, a diorama museum, a three-dimensional Vedic cinema, and a “House of Karmic Reactions.” Of course, as with any other project of this magnitude, we don’t expect it to manifest overnight, but we can try every day to make this dream a reality for your sweet pleasure, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s satisfaction, as well as for the sake of all Las Vegans’ spiritual benefit.

Therefore, we beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please continue blessing us with your causeless mercy so that we are able to accomplish everything and please you in this way. Thus we will remain always in the shelter of your divine lotus feet.

Your servants in Las Vegas, Nevada, USA.  
(written by Surapāla dāsa)

## Lisbon

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances. All glories to your feet of lotus!
