The unique opportunity to glorify our great spiritual master on this day of Vyāsa-pūjā is a tradition that is granted by our philosophy. But in order to properly pay our respect, we would like to analyze our relationship with Your Divine Grace, and thus begin by questioning ourselves: Are we sufficiently surrendered, or are we getting lost as time goes by? Because we encounter so many difficulties on our path to understanding your desires, at times we inquire: How surrendered are we? For this very reason we continue chanting our rounds and performing our duties, even amidst our limitations and difficulties.

Nonetheless, the challenge of our life as devotees and existence as human beings is to truly understand: “Who is Śrīla Prabhupāda?” And we believe that the only way to approach Kṛṣṇa consciousness is to first understand who you are and what your teachings are. To achieve this understanding, we must be sure and reassured of what you mean to us, of our understanding of your instructions, of our remembrance of your example, and last but not least, of our feelings for you.

Even though our vision is smeared with imperfections, we can measure the degree of our surrender by our effort to remain your servants, and in this way we will discover what you truly mean to us. We must be cautious not to fall into an illusion of who you are by developing egoistic interests, or by modifying your instructions in order to adjust them to our flickering circumstances.

Just as our devotional qualities naturally appear while practicing devotional service, Śrīla Prabhupāda consciousness also naturally appears as our Kṛṣṇa consciousness develops. Our Vyāsa-pūjā offering may be blemished by our defective reflections, but we pray that it is sufficiently valid to conduct us toward your consciousness, and that it may help us destroy the barriers that prevent us from glorifying you, surrendering to you, understanding you, and serving you twenty-four hours a day.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us understand that you are the best teacher of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes, the only and best author of the purports in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and the *Bhagavad-gītā,* and the giver of the most precious gift one can receive, the teachings of Lord Caitanya. Please help us understand that these realizations don’t occur by our effort but only by your causeless mercy.

Hoping to remain eternally glorifying you,  
Your humble servants in the ISKCON temple in Lisbon, Portugal,  
(written by Dhīra Kṛṣṇa dāsa)

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet on this 108thVyāsa-pūjā. All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for fulfilling the mission of Lord Caitanya.

On this most auspicious appearance day of Your Divine Grace, we are wondering where to begin to describe some of your unlimited glories. We have to confess that we have not even begun to realize our indebtedness to you. All we know is that the devotees of this temple are wholeheartedly trying to surrender to your *saṅkīrtana* mission, and by this endeavor we have had a glimpse of your ever-increasing mercy and its powerful effect on the fallen conditioned souls. If it were not for you, then souls such as we, while traversing this hellish city, would not have stumbled upon this vital soul-saving center and movement.

By your great sacrifice of coming to the West, you have given us a temple from which we are able to touch the whole world with love of God by distributing your transcendental books. We think that we are the most strategically placed center in ISKCON. We pray that you will send more devotees to add to the existing team of full-time book distributors in the center of Māyā’s kingdom. We pray that all devotees taking part in this movement take some time in their lives to taste the nectar for which they are always anxious, nectar that is directly available by distributing your books. We pray for your guidance and mercy to distribute more of your books to the fallen, conditioned souls and to be the prime example for the rest of ISKCON temples to follow.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so we can improve and develop our relationships among one another, especially between the temple devotees and the congregation. Without your mercy, we are helpless and lost. We can see how in your own life you were able to serve and care for all individuals, especially how you gave time to have loving relationships with your devotees. We pray and beg that, here at this temple, devotees of all statuses and *āśramas* can begin to inherit your abilities to enhance and develop loving relationships. This will enable us to ensure that all devotees who come here will go the full distance and never leave this Society.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, recently, with the help of a skilled facilitator, the temple leaders drew up a list of goals for the temple. We hope to achieve these goals by the year 2010. Please bless us with your causeless mercy so that we can make these dreams come true for the pleasure of pleasing you and ultimately Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. You taught us that we should not think small, so we beg for your help to empower us to succeed and expand your mission to new heights, and thus try to repay some of the debt we owe you.

**The Dream**

• To expand the facilities of ISKCON London and make them so prominent and attractive that the royal family will be pleased to attend

• To have one hundred full-time book distributors

• To make five new devotees each month

• To have twenty devotees in proper devotional attire going out six hours a day for *harināma* (two hundred devotees on Saturday nights)

• To have a Govinda’s Restaurant with table service, as well as a sandwich bar and a take-away

• To teach your *Bhagavad-gītā* in schools

• To have twenty-five thousand people attending Ratha-yātrā

• To have a Kṛṣṇa TV satellite channel

• To have one hundred thousand tourists visiting the temple every year

• To have the government declare Janmāṣṭamī a national holiday

• To have a Bhaktivedanta Leadership Center

• To have *prasādam* sold in large supermarkets such as Sainsbury’s and Tesco

• To educate all devotees in the practical application of the *Bhagavad-gītā* in their lives

• To have at least 108 strict congregational members cared for in a well-structured system

• To fill up the Patrons Board with members (about a hundred spaces are still empty)

• To offer “bridging” seminars and courses all day, seven days a week

• To care for temple residents and staff in a well-structured system

• To develop an unbreakably unified leadership team

Finally we, your humble servants, sincerely pray for your mercy on your auspicious 108thVyāsa-pūjā so that we can fulfill these goals and always be guided by you in our efforts to render pleasing service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Most of all, please give us the benediction that we can aspire to become the humble servants of your devotees.

All glories to Your Divine Grace!!

Your eternal servants at the temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, London, UK.  
(written by Bhakta Duncan Fisk)

## Malaga

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you! All glories to your divine mission! All glories to your instructions! All glories to your words! All glories to your books! And all glories to your Movement!

You said to us that everything is in your books. But just by mere reading they cannot be understood, because of their transcendental nature. They can be understood only by revelation within our hearts. This comes only when you are pleased upon us.

It’s like seeing a picture of the Lord or taking *prasādam* or chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra: one could be doing it for thousands of years and not realize its true meaning.

In our temple we are now working and praying to understand how you would have wanted us to live—to take full advantage of this life and also to help others. You have taught us how to do many transcendental activities, such as distributing *prasādam,* distributing your books, organizing Hare Kṛṣṇa festivals, taking care of the congregation, going out for *harināma,* and many other activities to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the planet and attract many people.

However, what is your ultimate plan? Is it to maintain these people all their lives in the association of other devotees so they will never desire to go back to material society?

You have brought us a Society, a full civilization, not a new temple, church, or a mosque. But we have failed for so many years to understand this point, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please instruct us from within on how to do it!

We are suffering so much because we don’t know! So many devotees, after chanting *japa* for so many years, have gone to live outside the temple, and after a year or so they are again influenced by Kali-yuga society—TV, new car, videos, money, etc. Therefore we are searching and searching for the lost “S” in your ISKCON! Please kindly reveal it to us!

On occasion you commented on Śrīla Bhaktivinoda by saying, in effect, “He was a perfect Vaiṣṇava, and therefore he could have spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, but he kindly left that work for us, and the credit also.”

In the same way, you are a perfect Vaiṣṇava and also could have created a perfect society that would have completely replaced the material one, but in your pastime of creating this movement you left some of that work for us. We are trying to come closer to the conception of living you crafted for us. You said that we have to give the devotees as much Kṛṣṇa conscious happiness as possible, that our managers have to know well the necessities of the devotees.

You taught us that the four basic material needs are eating, sleeping, mating, and defending. You teach in your books that these needs can be easily met if we have a piece of land and cultivate our own food, so you had us buy farms all over the world.

Concerning mental needs, that is, education and development of skills, you said that all our ISKCON centers have to be educational centers, where our devotees can learn to become perfect *brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas,* teachers, *svāmīs,* and spiritual masters according to each one’s capacity.

The social needs, that is, to assemble with family and friends and share the same thoughts, are also easily satisfied by the building of organized villages in open spaces, with the Hare Kṛṣṇa temple as the center of the village.

Finally and most important is the spiritual need, which is the need for self-realization and the development of love for Kṛṣṇa. This is the essence of all needs, and it is what gives sense and value to our lives. Satisfying this need should be the only goal of our human life, which is different from that of the animals.

You have also completely fulfilled this necessity of human society by giving perfect descriptions of the Supreme Personality of Godhead—His qualities, names, pastimes, etc.—especially in wonderful books like *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and *Bhagavad-gītā*.

One thing we are seeing in our *yātrā*is that although the system of a temple president, temple commander, and treasurer, all supervised by the GBC representative, is nice for the management of the temple, to organize a fully functioning society we have to follow Kṛṣṇa’s instruction in *Bhagavad-gītā* 4.13: *cātur-varṇyaṁ mayā sṛṣṭam*. You also say that *varṇāśrama* is the law and that we should not break the law. Of course it is all in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Therefore we are trying to work by “squeezing our brain” to organize and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere, fully aware of the following injunction, which you taught us: *kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana—*only the mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu can bring success to our efforts. So we fall at His divine lotus feet through your divine lotus feet. Please accept our humble service and be pleased upon us.

Your eternal servants in Malaga, Spain.

## Manchester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The battalion boat hits the enemy-lined shore. Making their way up the beach, dodging the rapid fire, a few soldiers make it through the barrage to establish an outpost. It seems some soldiers have previously done battle here and have either died or lived to move onto other fighting frontiers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in Manchester, England, some of your grand-disciples are trying to relate to your fitting war analogies that you would use in your lectures, purports, morning walks, and room conversations. Applying your analogies to our situation here, we are, by your guidance, slowly but surely grasping what it means to actually fight.

Your resplendent *mūrti* resides in this small urban ashram with a temple room and altar. Your presence continually reminds us that each day is a new surrender and fighting frontier.

Though we may be inexperienced in “fighting the fight” and plagued by both individual and collective impediments, a comforting air of encouragement pervades the hearts of the *saṅkīrtana* devotees here, who all think, “Prabhupāda is in charge.”

Knowing this, as we attempt to preach we take solace in seeing so many impediments and bad elements overcome. This is mainly due to the fact that your original desire to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the world is the foundation that empowers your disciples and grand-disciples to “do the needful.”

You have given us everything—the weapons, the bunkers, the established regiment, the servant leaders and staff, but most importantly the inspiration, vision, and impetus to fight on, not only in our individual lives but for the collective army also.

O general of generals,  
Your instruction issues forth,  
Giving your foot soldiers  
More strength than they are worth.  
We realize we are nothing without your mighty call.  
O Prabhupāda, give us power to overcome Māyā once and “for all.”

“For all” is a motto that continues to ring in your consistent preaching approach.

To further the war analogy, generally wars take place to establish the supremacy of one view over another. In physical terms, a victorious warring side would protect its citizens and weaken and defeat the opposing side, who would either surrender or retreat.

We grand-disciples are the weak citizens you are protecting. You are spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that *all* living beings can receive Lord Caitanya’s mercy.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are truly “for all” in this fight against Māyā.

“For all” is ingrained in every word and action that emits from your transcendental person. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, show us how to imbibe this mood so we too can “fight for all.”

Please help us realize your presence at all times.

If you hadn’t come to an “enemy-lined shore” and established an “outpost,” where would we be? Again, we ask this question with heartfelt awe: Where would we be? To hear that you would shed tears when told of modern Westerners wasting valuable time in frivolous sports and nonsense activities is to hear of the real compassionate activities of a true saint who is living the real purport of *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī:* feeling pain at seeing others in pain.

We beg to assist in fulfilling Lord Caitanya’s desire that His name be chanted in every town and village.

Manchester, an previous battleground where other ISKCON soldiers fought who have since moved on, is again receiving the weapons you have given us in the form of books, *harināma,* and *prasādam*. With those transcendental weapons we are attempting to establish a base camp for cultivating, recruiting, and training fallen souls, and in this way hope to increasing and mobilize your army.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in our tiny attempt to give you great pleasure, the devotees of Manchester humbly beg to be of some use to you in this fight to set the fallen souls free.

We beg to assist in fulfilling Lord Caitanya’s desire that Kṛṣṇa consciousness spread to every town and village, under the inspirational guidance and leadership of your compassionate son, Devāmṛta Swami.

All glories to your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious 108thanniversary of your appearance.

*Your servants in Manchester, UK.*

## Manipur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our most humble *praṇāmas* at your divine lotus feet. On this most auspicious 108th anniversary of your appearance day, O beloved divine grandfather, Jagad Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda, we, the insignificant servants of our spiritual master (His Holiness Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami), GBC of ISKCON Manipur, have taken this rare opportunity to express a few words to be offered to your divine lotus feet.

In this Age of Kali, the age of quarrel and hypocrisy, Your Divine Grace planted on this globe, almost forty years ago, a precious devotional desire tree (*kalpa-taru*) in the form of ISKCON. Due to the unlimited blessings of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandra, this tree has now grown up fully. Under the care of your beloved disciples, ISKCON is now giving shelter to the people of the Kali age in various forms, such as faith to the faithless, direction to the bewildered, knowledge to the ignorant, and strength to the weak. In other words, your movement is giving spiritual water to the spiritually thirsty, spiritual food to the spiritually hungry, and spiritual medicine to the spiritually sick.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you wanted to transform this earthly planet into a Vaikuṇṭha-dhāma (Goloka Vṛndāvana.) You wanted every class of people to be Kṛṣṇa conscious, to demonstrate an ideal example of a noble and civilized human society. There may be differences of sex, caste, creed, language, and religion amongst us; still, we are all human beings, children of the Supreme Lord. Due to these differences of language and faith, there may be various names attributed to God, but there is only one God. God, Bhagavān, can never be plural.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you wanted to show that all human beings should develop Vaiṣṇava qualities. A Vaiṣṇava is one who knows the absolute values, meaning, and purpose of life. The *śāstras* (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 20.108) define the ultimate identity of the soul:

*jīvera ‘svarūpa’ haya—kṛṣṇera ‘nitya-dāsa’*

In any position, the living entity is the eternal servant of the servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. We have nothing to do with our temporary designations. This was the clear instruction of Lord Caitanya. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught the same principle to your disciples and followers.

It may now rightly be said that in Manipur there is no one high or low, irrespective of caste, creed, color, name, fame, prestige, or position, who has not heard the name of ISKCON. “Hare Kṛṣṇa” has now become a form of address commonly used to greet friends or strangers. This is all due to your matchless devotional activities, Śrīla Prabhupāda, which are always transcendental. This is the mercy Your Divine Grace has shown to the people of Kali-yuga.

By your divine mercy, we are abiding by your divine instruction, “First become the servant of your spiritual master; then you will be able to understand these conclusions.”

“Whatever little knowledge you have, whatever ability to write, to speak, to tackle a problem—all this and whatever else you have should be utilized for the service of the Lord. In this way Śrīla Prabhupāda will be extremely pleased.” How forceful and educational this divine instruction is! This is the instruction given constantly by our spiritual master, His Holiness Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami, to his disciples. How dear he is to Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how merciful are your divine blessings upon us! You have bestowed your beloved disciple His Holiness Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami as our spiritual master. How fortunate we are!

This is your greatest divine blessing upon us, the fallen insignificant servants of your servants.

The most lowly servants of your servants in Manipur, India.

## Medie

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Your Divine Grace has made the mercy of Kṛṣṇa accessible to everyone, including us here, who are the most fallen, degraded *mlecchas*.

This is a special mercy, unparalleled in the history of religion in the world. Great personalities have come but could not achieve this feat of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, or love of Godhead, all over the world. But, Śrīla Prabhupāda, this you achieved in a little more than a decade, even though you were, in the material sense, an old man!

Now, by this singular effort of yours the whole world is drenched in the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We only see a very, very bleak and confused lot of people here in Africa. What hope would there be for them or us if you had not risked your life, even at a very advanced age, to go beyond the shores of India and inaugurate the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and inundate the whole world with the bliss of Kṛṣṇa consciousness!

Today, in this remotest part of the world called Medie, near Accra, in Ghana, we have a beautiful temple and have installed Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda. We have Their *darśana* every day and are trying to serve Them the best we can. We also have a *gośāla* and a school. Our clinic project is almost completed, and the devotees are always on traveling *harināma,* distributing the holy name and spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya as you have directed and personally propagated.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is our great fortune and privilege to be recipients of as well as participants in this great Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Again, how could we have been so blessed, if not for your causeless mercy? Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternally indebted to you. Please bless us to remain the eternal servants of your servants.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Yours unworthy servants at ISKCON’s Rādhā-Govinda temple in Medie, Ghana.  
(written by Jagadvāsu dāsa)

## Melbourne

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Thank you for your tireless service example; undaunted by setbacks, unfazed by success. Thank you for your gifts.

Preaching is sometimes not pleasant work. We experience setbacks. Friends abandon us. Enemies attack. Our inadequacies stall us. Mistakes of the past seek us out.

Yet despite the difficulties and even though Kṛṣṇa Himself warns us not to preach to the faithless, still we are inspired.

So that is our proposal. Although we are warned not to speak to the rascals, but still, we are flattering, “My dear sir, please hear, please hear, please hear. Please give up this habit. Please do it. \[*Bhagavad-gītā* class, Ahmedabad, 9 December 1972\]

Despite the hardship we like to preach.

So to turn one man to Kṛṣṇa consciousness we have to shed hundred tons of blood. So therefore it is warned. We are not easy-going, that not to preach. We have taken the risk to preach. And it has, it is becoming successful. People will take it. So although Kṛṣṇa warns, “Don’t speak to these classes of men,” we take the risk.

Even though we are warned.

So this warning of Kṛṣṇa is all right, but a pure devotee, in spite of warning of Kṛṣṇa, they approach the *atapaskāya, abhaktāya,* and flatter them: “Please take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” That we should do. Kṛṣṇa will be pleased.

Our austerities pale in comparison to yours. Yet there is immense pleasure in making progress in our personal advancement and preaching. The effort is always worth it despite the result seeming far away.

Thank you for providing the means of success. We are able to succeed only by accepting the gifts given to us by you.

The gift of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa

There is no greater pleasure to be had than from chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. There is no benefit higher than freedom from the tyranny of the mind. There is nothing more relishable than chanting sixteen rounds.

The gift of Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam

Completely rejecting all religious activities which are materially motivated, this *Bhāgavata Purāṇa* propounds the highest truth, which is understandable by those devotees who are fully pure in heart. The highest truth is reality distinguished from illusion for the welfare of all. Such truth uproots the threefold miseries. This beautiful *Bhāgavatam,* compiled by the great sage Vyāsadeva \[in his maturity\], is sufficient in itself for God realization. What is the need of any other scripture? As soon as one attentively and submissively hears the message of *Bhāgavatam,* by this culture of knowledge the Supreme Lord is established within his heart. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.1.2\]

The gift of prasādam

How can I begin to describe the relish of a meal of remnants of food offered to the Lord?

The gift of Deity worship

How fortunate we are to dedicate our lives’ activities to the service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhā-vallabha?

The gift of an ISKCON temple

What better place to live in the world?

The gift of association with devotees

The test of our advancement is realized in the association of devotees. We test our advancement in sharing *bhakti* with others. We are reminded of our faults and weaknesses and the need to push ever forward in our pursuit of pure devotion by association, not only with the nondevotional but especially with the like-minded.

We relish the items of *bhakti* by sharing them with all.

The gift of preaching

This includes the matchless gift of the difficulties we encounter on the path of spiritual life. By embracing them we are guaranteed to become humble, to appreciate our actual position and our dependence on the Lord for guidance, inspiration, and mercy. In so doing we appreciate your efforts more and more. How much of a father you are to us! How much you tolerated in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all!

I feel a deep sense of disqualification. Am I really willing to help others? Despite that sense of inability, I am determined to act. Despite the difficulties, your example inspires us to try. Your gifts give us the strength to do it.

Your servants in Melbourne, Australia.  
(written by Aniruddha dāsa)

## Mexico City

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

For many years the ISKCON temple in Mexico City, which you visited twice and blessed with the touch of your lotus feet, was in legal danger of being lost. Just a few months ago most of your followers here were losing hope. However, your prayers invoked the mercy of the Lord and inspired Him to give us a second chance. As long as you see your determined disciples inspiring new generations to continue the *saṅkīrtana-yajña,* your mercy overfloods and blesses your children and grandchildren even more.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day we want to pray that you force us to use our lives in your service and expand this golden mission of Lord Caitanya. Without the systematic spreading of the holy name by your determined followers, the whole of humanity will only suffer in agony, quitting their bodies in desperation and fear, not knowing our eternal occupation of serving Lord Kṛṣṇa.

We want to thank you for honoring us by letting us participate in your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. This is such a precious and glorious opportunity that we will never be able to estimate the benefit we are getting.

We remain grateful and graced by your mercy.

This time we will fight with all our hearts.  
Your servants in Mexico City, Mexico.

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On February 25, 1975, you blessed the devotees in South Florida by visiting the Miami temple. Pleased at seeing our newly installed Gaura-Nitāi Deities, during your arrival address you urged us to accept the blessings of Lord Caitanya and to perfect our lives by helping to propagate the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Under your order, we have worked for many years to do this. We have gained great strength from our firm faith that you have given us everything we need to become pure devotees of the Lord and to guide the rest of the world on the spiritual path. We realize that there is absolutely no need to ever wander outside of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness for anything spiritual or material.

*Śrī Īśopaniṣad* (Invocation) states,

*oṁ pūrṇam adaḥ pūrṇam idaṁ pūrṇāt pūrṇam udacyate*  
*pūrṇasya pūrṇam ādāya pūrṇam evāvaśiṣyate*

**“The Personality of Godhead is perfect and complete, and because He is completely perfect, all emanations from Him, such as this phenomenal world, are perfectly equipped as complete wholes. Whatever is produced of the Complete Whole is also complete in itself. Because He is the Complete Whole, even though so many complete units emanate from Him, He remains the complete balance.”**

Lord Brahmā, under the direct guidance of Lord Kṛṣṇa, created this material universe, which is complete in itself. Similarly, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, directly under Lord Kṛṣṇa’s guidance, created the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, a spiritual society, which is complete in itself. That means there is no need to search outside of ISKCON to advance in spiritual life.

The philosophical basis for all of our activities is in your books. You said, “There is nothing new to be said. Everything is in my books. Therefore, if you want to know me, read my books.” In your books you have armed us with everything that we need to become Kṛṣṇa conscious and to carry out your mission.

Although you aspired to complete the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and other literatures, you also said that you had already given us all the philosophy in your purports of the First Canto of the *Bhāgavatam*. Regarding your own spiritual master, you once said,

My Guru Mahārāja explained *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* the first verse, *janmādy asya yataḥ,* in Dacca, for three months. Actually, it is so profound. *Janmādy asya yataḥ*. This very quote contains all the Vedānta philosophy.

Also, you did not write your books like an ordinary author who is subject to the four defects. In fact, you said, “I didn’t write these books; they were dictated to me by Kṛṣṇa.”

You knew that you had given us everything we needed to perfect our lives in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and that if we would strictly follow your instructions Lord Kṛṣṇa would continue to protect our budding devotional creepers. You expressed this in 1967 prior to returning to India to recover from a heart attack. You rejected a disciple’s proposal to bring one of your Godbrothers to stand in for you while you were gone and, if the worst were to happen, take over leadership of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. You said, “If this person speaks just one word different from what I am speaking, there will be great confusion among you.”

Therefore, we pray that we may remain fully devoted to the International Society for Krishna Consciousness as you established it, and that we dedicate our lives to the propagation of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Your humble servants at the ISKCON temple in Miami, Florida, USA.

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You are the best servitor of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. You are very dear to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

You are the great architect who devised the master plan to rescue the world from the tight grip of impersonalism and atheism. By your mercy only has Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s prediction that the Holy Name would spread to all towns and villages become realized. You have removed the darkness of ignorance from the innermost core of our hearts and given us the means to liberate ourselves from the binding shackles of birth and death. On the anniversary of your appearance in this world, we gather at your feet with tears in our eyes and pray, “O Śrīla Prabhupāda, our savior, please empower us to demonstrate our gratitude to you by our efforts to firmly establish the worldwide spiritual society that was the object of your constant meditation. May the cheating religions of the meat-eaters crumble to insignificance under the weight of the sins they commit in the name of our Lord. And let the conditioned souls find relief from the sufferings of the times by taking shelter of you.”

Unlike the false teachers who work by the wiles of Kali, you re-established the principles of religion in the world by your own exemplary behavior. You did not teach imaginary religious principles born of the influence of *aviśuddha-buddhaya,* erroneous intelligence. You reiterated instead the wisdom of perseverant practice of theistic conduct guided by scriptures, as taught by Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to Śrīla Rūpa and Sanātana Gosvāmīs. Because you chanted the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* without any of the inebrieties of *sahajiyā*impersonalism and covered atheism, you have become the emblem of a generation, the living proof of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s declaration that “Whatever action a great man performs, common men follow. And whatever standards he sets by his own exemplary acts, the entire world pursues.”(*Bhagavad-gītā* 3.21) As we stumble like some unruly army, trying to follow in your giant footsteps, we fervently pray that we may practice and transmit to others the standards you taught: Books are the basis; Purity is the force; Preaching is the essence; Utility is the principle. Please inspire us from within in such a manner that our chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare may become free of the ten offenses to the Holy Name. Only then shall we rightfully claim to be your followers, observing Lord Caitanya’s dictum that “A teacher should behave properly before he begins teaching.”

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Montreal, Canada.

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, the devotees of the Rādhā-Gopīnātha Temple in the Chowpatty district of Mumbai, are eternally grateful to you for having expressed in 1971 your desire (*saṅkalpa*) to have a temple here. You expressed this desire to your secretary, Śyāmasundara Prabhu, while walking down the stairs of the Bharati Vidya Bhavan. It appears that Kṛṣṇa has gladly empowered us to help fulfill your desire.

You have also kindly showed us by your personal example the best ways of practicing *bhakti,* namely the nine processes taught by Śrī Prahlāda Mahārāja, which are quite esoteric. Since you yourself were the best listener (always hearing *śāstra*), you taught us all how to attentively hear. You were the best *kīrtana* leader, even when you were ill, so we get so inspired to see you lead the *kīrtana* party (in films), glorifying the holy name. You also taught us how to always remember Kṛṣṇa (*viṣṇu-smaraṇam*) while doing all our routine duties, such as cooking, honoring *prasādam*, etc. You always performed exquisite *arcanam* and *vandanam bhakti,* and you personally trained your disciples in decorating the *arcā-vigraha* (*śṛṅgāra*) and in performing *ārati, pūjā,* and fire sacrifices for initiations and marriages, thus purifying thousands of lives.

You often gave the example of Hanumān as the best exponent of pure service to the Lord (*dāsyam*) because of his unbreakable determination to help Rāma rescue Sītā. In fact, you did a similar service for Kṛṣṇa by rescuing Lakṣmī (Sītā) from the grip of rascals across the oceans (from the USA, Europe, Japan, etc.) and engaging her in the service of the Lord by spending the money to develop spectacular projects in Bombay, Vṛndāvana, and Māyāpur. Your service to the order of your spiritual master is the most exemplary motivation for all of us.

In your poem to Kṛṣṇa just before landing in the USA, you expressed your mood of longing to your dearest friend, Kṛṣṇa (*sakhyam*): “When will the day come when I will again frolic with You in the fields of Vṛndāvana?” You gave us a clear picture of all the qualities of Kṛṣṇa, His holy *dhāma,* His paraphernalia, His *līlā,* etc., which only an intimate friend would be privy to.

Your service to Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur *dhāmas* is in the category of *pāda-sevanam*. You put these *dhāmas* on the international map and glorified them to inspire us to continue serving them.

Your entire life was a glorious example of *ātma-nivedanam*. Whereas Bali Mahārāja set an example of this form of *bhakti,* you have benefited millions of souls now and in the future by your utter surrender to your Guru Mahārāja and Lord Kṛṣṇa. Hence you are an authorized *śakty-āveśāvatāra* who really implemented the wishes of Lord Gaurāṅga and the *guru-paramparā*.

We once again offer our repeated obeisances and gratitude to you and pray that your mercy keeps flowing to encourage us to become more Kṛṣṇa conscious and make others also devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir in Chowpatty, Mumbai, India.  
(written by Śrī Nāthjī dāsa)

## Mumbai (Mira Road)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

We have learned from your disciples that ISKCON is your body and you are its soul. The natural course of events in Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārī Temple (ISKCON Mira Road) is proof that you are in total control of all situations in reviving this part of your body. Because of our immaturity and many faults, we have created lot of trouble and pain for you over the years. Somehow, because of your patience, you have been tolerating us simply because you know for certain that the conditioned souls have no hope other than your shelter in the form of this transcendental society, ISKCON. It is you who are preaching and we who are your blunt tools. The troubles we undergo in being your tools are nothing but a sharpening process and a cleansing process. Your instruction “Whatever may happen, never leave ISKCON” has saved us all from going back toward illusion.

As you know, this part of your body, i.e., ISKCON Mira Road, was paralyzed till recently because of being oblivious of your mood and mission. The major breakthrough achieved this year is that we have got a direction and a vision to accomplish your mission. By your transcendental arrangement we have an integrated approach for accomplishing the task of building a beautiful temple for your pleasure. The most important accomplishment during this year was to establish the faith in the leadership among the *brahmacārīs* and congregational devotees. Under the able leadership of His Holiness Gopāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami, we have set up the management and standard operating procedures that are strong and workable, which is the basis of executing the task of building the temple. We have cleared all debts, which was our prime concern, as the debts were bringing a bad name to your society.

The Life Membership department, which was practically dead, has been given life by His Grace Janmāṣṭamī dāsa. We now understand the value of devotees, and we hope his return to your society is most pleasing to you. We beg your mercy so that our hard hearts will become softer and softer to accommodate anyone with even a bit of sincere desire to serve your mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel ashamed to show our face because we were not able to complete the *āśrama* work. But we hope that you, as a compassionate father, will forgive us for all our lethargy. The construction work has been started again, and we promise that it will go on without stoppage. His Grace Nanda-kiśora dāsa and His Grace Jagajīvana dāsa have been given the exclusive responsibility of completing the *āśrama*.

You have said that the *brahmacārīs* are the legs of our society, and by your mercy you are sending us many *brahmacārīs*. As you considered that Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura sent you so many sincere and dedicated souls so you could accomplish his mission, we consider that you are sending us *bhaktas* so we can accomplish your mission. Please give us the strength to take care of them nicely so that they can be future commanders of your society. We consider that it is your special mercy on His Grace Sītā Mohan dāsa, for he has been chosen by you to be a trustworthy son who can play the role of a mother.

Even in the eyes of big multinationals like *The Times of India* your ISKCON stands out as a unique and trustworthy organization. They called us and offered to donate some land, a small house, and a partly constructed hospital in Vrajeswari (a forty-five-minute drive from Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārī Mandir). Mr. Badsha, the landlord of the place, just gave it to us. We propose to develop it and use it for conducting yoga retreats and camps and for other preaching purposes. This shows the incredible amount of good will you and your society have won, even in the eyes of materialists. Śrīmatī Meena Ben Shah, an esteemed judge and a well-wisher of ISKCON, did a great job of getting the land out of all legal complications

The acquisition of Śrīnāthjī Dhāma as an ally of ISKCON Mira Road heralds a new direction toward the expansion of your movement here. A beautiful *gośālā* and *gurukula* are planned, along with projects for preaching to tribes in and around Mumbai. Already a few tribal children chant your *pranāma* mantra and Hare Kṛṣṇa, along with a few *ślokas* from the *Bhagavad-gītā*.

His Grace Harsha Govind Prabhu, with his extraordinary wit and influence, is about to get the present land clearance from the U.L.C. authorities. Due to the space constraints in the present land, we were on the lookout for a bigger piece of land, and by your transcendental arrangement we got a very good piece of land of about 2.25 acres. The landlords had proposed to build a temple of Lord Śrī Rāmacandra there. Somehow that piece of land came to us, along with carved stones dumped there for the proposed Rāma temple. This actually shows that the Lord, by dint of His transcendental independence, wants to be served only by you. We very much understand that your choice is the best choice, and so we chose Śrī Prem Nāthjī as our temple architect. The *āśrama* work is in the final stages, and the *brahmacārīs* will soon be getting a proper facility for preaching the glories of the Lord.

His Grace Nanda Kishore Prabhu has worked hard to get the road in front of the temple renamed Bhaktivedanta Swami Marg.

There has been a tremendous increase in the standard of Deity worship. This is all because you have inspired His Grace Rasamandal Prabhu and Madhumati Mātājī through your inconceivable potency. His Grace Kumbha dāsa Prabhu, known for his consistency, is making sure your dear Lord is taken care of very nicely. The artistic excellence of His Grace Sura dāsa Prabhu has made the present temple, which is nothing but a shed, look like a palace.

We are very conscious of this divine warning: “If there is no preaching, the temple will become a *godown*.” Preaching is increasing in all areas. Your temple, which was earlier known for its congregational preaching, is also developing its youth preaching under the guidance of Kamala-locana dāsa and Kundal Kṛṣṇa dāsa. With a lot of children’s programs happening, this temple has become a home for many families residing in and around Mira Road. All the credit goes to Śrī Kṛṣṇan and Śrīmatī Bhavānī Kṛṣṇan, along with Kṣīrodakaśāyī-devī dāsī.

The Bhisma Dept. is gradually developing by the sincere perseverance of His Grace Vanamālī Prabhu. The Saṅkīrtana Dept., headed by His Grace Sarvāṅga Prabhu and Matsya Priya Prabhu, is doing very well, and last marathon we distributed a record 22,500 *Gītās*.

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we pray at your lotus feet for just one thing: Please give us more service and the strength to accomplish it. We conclude our offering with a pledge that next year’s offering will not be void of the report about starting the construction of our new temple project.

Your servants at the ISKCON temple at Mira Road, Mumbai, India.  
(written by Sura dāsa)

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the 108th anniversary of your appearance, we are submitting at your feet a humble report of our center. By your mercy and the inspiration of your dear disciple His Holiness Lokanāth Swami Mahārāja, we successfully conducted our third annual Ratha-yātrā on 7 February 2004. In fact, now we have our own chariot, which we can lend to other centers in Vidarbha. Also, the best news is that we have acquired our own land in Nagpur, at a place called Watora, in Wardhman Nagar, after almost two decades of patient waiting. Very soon we will have a preaching center there, and this year we are hopefully going to celebrate Janmāṣṭamī on the new land. This is happening purely by your causeless mercy upon us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, recently Nagpur has been declared the site of a future international airport. The city is likely to have an international cargo hub as well. This means Nagpur is becoming cosmopolitan, and so there will arise a need for more and more preaching. Please bless us to conduct these preaching programs successfully.

We also have an ISKCON Youth Forum in Nagpur, which is gradually progressing. We hope that some youth can become part of our preaching movement.

We sincerely admit that we are lacking in the following departments: book distribution, Food for Life, and congregational preaching. The spirit is willing but the flesh is weak.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this special day we beg you humbly to grant us the intelligence to overcome our shortcomings in these areas.

Please bless us with more committed *brahmacārīs* in the temple to propagate the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Aspiring to be your good servants,  
Your devotees at the ISKCON center in Nagpur, India.

## New Braja Mandala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please allow us to place our tiny hearts at the dust of your delicate lotus feet, which are circumambulating the whole world.

We are aware that we never deserved your association, and thus most of us never met you in person. We are aware that despite all our weaknesses you are nonetheless giving us far more than what we deserve (namely, nil). Yet despite our understanding these facts, and although we feel happy with all the wonders you are mercifully bestowing upon these beggars of your causeless mercy, we are still hankering for more, and this hankering causes us to feel sad . . . and also hopeful!

The dust of your lotus feet is like the shiny clouds of dust caused by Lord Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma while returning from the pasturing fields accompanied by Their calves and friends. Upon seeing these clouds of dust, the mothers, young *gopīs,* and other residents of Vṛndāvana would become motionless. Similarly, from our low and humble state of dedication, we also find our hearts thrilled by the sight of the shiny clouds of dust caused by your walking around the world. This shiny dust remains present by the divine grace inundating the hearts of your fervent, loving servants, who are surrendered, compassionate, merciful, enthusiastic, and extremely bold while allowing us to experience the glories of your divine and most loving grace by tasting, handling, and showing us these divine glories themselves.

And what is this hope that keeps us alive? This is your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, which enables us to find newer ways to serve you; and thus our heartfelt desire is to become useful instruments of your wonderful plan for New Braja Maṇḍala now unfolding before of us. Therefore, on this most auspicious day of your appearance, we beg you to bless us with the ability to properly serve your servants, with the ability to please them, with the understanding—like a loving, blooming flower—to worship them as they so deserve. And please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us with the capacity to receive their love in the mood of pleasing Your Divine Grace.

Fervently desiring to remain the servants of your servants,  
The devotees serving you in the home of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda-candra, in New Braja Maṇḍala, Spain.

## New Dvārakā

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

I have been asked by Svavāsa Prabhu, your Los Angeles temple president, to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you on behalf of the temple community. Although I feel unqualified, I have agreed write on his request, and so I beg your forgiveness for any shortcomings.

As you know, you spent more time at one or another of your Los Angeles temples than at any other of your Western temples. You visited Los Angeles eleven times, and you would refer to New Dvārakā as your world headquarters.

Recently your loving disciple Nṛsiṁhānanda dāsa compiled a complete set of videos of Your Divine Grace. It contains a wonderful clip of you leaving Los Angeles. You are sitting at the airport waiting for your flight, and the devotees have offered you four or five garlands, which you have graciously accepted. As the devotees ecstatically perform *kīrtana* for your pleasure, you take off one garland after another, pull off the flowers one by one, and lovingly throw them to the devotees. Everyone is in great bliss!

Such are the exchanges you have with your followers. We offer you the fragrant flowers of all our love, and Your Divine Grace reciprocates with the flowers of your love and mercy, which allow us to grow in devotion and love you more and more.

This exchange continues in many forms. And one way of looking at your ISKCON is as a field of activity that nourishes this exchange of love with you. Out of your unequaled love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa and your compassion for the suffering conditioned souls, including us, you have given us engagement in the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. And we try to return this love with our efforts to develop ISKCON according to your vision.

Recently, some of your North American leaders have been gathering regularly here in Los Angeles, drawing inspiration from this hallowed *yātrā* to plan how to work together more closely and intelligently to please you. We feel it is time to recommit ourselves to your mood of dynamic expansion. To do this, we feel we need to first rekindle the enthusiasm we had when Your Divine Grace was physically present with us. We are focusing this expansion, for now, on developing new preaching activities that will complement existing temples, projects, and communities. We pray that society at large will respond favorably to our efforts. We remember how bold you were in taking risks for Kṛṣṇa. We also remember how you always measured our successes and failures, and so we wish to measure ourselves with your yardsticks. With a desire to please you, we are working toward an expanded and better-trained clergy, more and higher-quality programs and facilities for ISKCON’s congregation and the public at large, increased quantity and quality of book distribution, larger and more committed congregations, and increased income for all temples and projects.

Individually, we your disciples all feel a growing sense of urgency. Many of us are acutely aware of impending old age and daily ask ourselves, How can I best spend my remaining years? Some of your powerful preachers have already departed. Many of us are feeling the limits imposed by infirmity. Aware of the many instructions you imparted and your vision of what ISKCON should be, we often feel we have not done all you wish us to. And we feel the biological clock ticking louder and louder each day, counting down our remaining time in this body.

With whatever time remains to us, how can we best insure that your legacy will be preserved? And as part of that legacy, how do we solidify the fundamental principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you taught us while at the same time creating a capacity in ISKCON for applying these principles in an ever-changing social and cultural environment as the movement goes forward? There are cornerstones to be laid, concepts to be established, principles to be understood, infrastructures to be built, and much more to be done to insure ISKCON’s strong growth in the future.

Our understanding of your vision of the future may sometimes be unclear. Our capacity for foresight may be weakened by our necessary concentration on current issues. Yet we recall that when you had only the 26 Second Avenue temple, you printed business cards that said “Centers Throughout The World.” You never thought small, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and indeed you once said that your inability to do so was your “disease”! So how do we discern what your vision for ISKCON is now? What should ISKCON look like twenty years from now, two hundred years from now? What are the details of this picture that right now seems a bit hazy to us? Please help us clearly see your vision of ISKCON’s future.

You taught us to have an international perspective, that the International Society for Krishna Consciousness is meant for establishing the precepts of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu worldwide. How do we do this? How do we meet our local responsibilities and at the same time collaborate for pushing on the worldwide preaching mission?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us your vision of the next two or three hundred years. What elements should we put into place now that will bear fruit far in the future? What will the devotees of ISKCON two hundred years hence think of us—this band of your *dīkṣā*disciples whom you asked to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness on your behalf? What should we do now that will help them live according to your core instructions? Who should the next generation of leaders be, and how should they be trained? How can we best insure that these future leaders do not make the same mistakes we did?

These are some of the questions we place before you to evoke your guidance and inspiration. Fortunately—supremely fortunately—we always have your instructions in your books, lectures, letters, and conversations. And by following these instructions, we can learn to listen to the further instructions and inspiration you are imparting to us in our hearts.

This point is very clear from your Concluding Words to your translation of the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta:*

I still consider His Divine Grace to be always present with me by his *vāṇī,* his words. . . . I think that His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura is always seeing my activities and guiding me within my heart by his words. As it is said in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, tene brahma hṛdā ya ādi-kavaye*. Spiritual inspiration comes from within the heart, wherein the Supreme Personality of Godhead, in His Paramātmā feature, is always sitting with all His devotees and associates.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have full faith that you are seeing our activities and guiding us from within and without. We beseech you to continue to extend your mercy to us. Please help us surrender ourselves utterly to you so we can become your successful agents in fulfilling your preaching mission.

Your servants at the New Dvārakā devotee community, Los Angeles, California, USA.  
(written by Gopāl Bhaṭṭa dāsa)

## New Goloka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

As a most confidential servitor of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa, you are also supremely glorious: *sākṣād-dharitvena*. Just as Mother Ganges is best worshiped by an offering of her own sacred water, we, your fortunate and grateful followers, can best glorify you by trying to understand and glorify your unequalled service to the divine mission of mercy of the *karuṇāvatara,* Lord Śrī Caitanyadeva. Because of your perfect *guru-niṣṭhā,* your unconditional faith in the words of your own great master, you are forever empowered to successfully carry out his order to bless the whole world with *ananya-bhakti,* pure devotion to God. Thus you have demonstrated for the whole world that the essence of Vaiṣṇavism is this utter fidelity to the fulfillment of *gaura-vāṇī*: sacrificing everything for the uplifting of mankind.

Within the greater *acyuta-gotra,* you have a very prominent and specific service. Specially chosen by Lord Gaurāṅga and His associates to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness on a global scale, you are empowered to fulfill Their munificent desire. When you sat down under a tree in New York’s Tompkins Square Park to chant for the pleasure of your masters, you were able to bring to that time and place the greatest force in the universe—*gaura-śakti,* the transcendental potency of Caitanya Mahāprabhu—to awaken all sincere souls to their real self as eternal servants of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. By your humility and surrender, you were thus able to ignite the Hare Kṛṣṇa explosion, whose shock waves are still spreading all around the world in an unprecedented manifestation of divine mercy. We are all witnesses and participants in this ongoing miracle of Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s expansion of the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Now it is our duty to assist you in your service to propagate *śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana* by simply preserving your legacy, your perfect teachings, “as it is.” Within your all-encompassing teachings (your books, lectures, talks, etc.) is preserved, in its most succinct and accessible presentation, the complete legacy of Lord Caitanya’s empowered successors, the purest *bhāgavata-dharma: śuddha-bhakti,* selfless service to God. Your *vāṇī* is thus *pūrṇam,* covering the beginning, middle, and end of the *bhakti-mārga*. Hearing and propagating this divine message of Godhead is the service program for all generations of ISKCON devotees.

We, your followers and servants at New Gokula, pray that you kindly bless us with ever-increasing sincerity and dedication to more effectively assist you in your eternal service to the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Your grateful servants at New Gokula, ISKCON’s temple in Vancouver, Canada.  
(written by Gokulānanda dāsa)

## New Goloka II

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*kṛṣṇa-tattva, bhakti-tattva, prema-tattva sāra*  
*bhāva-tattva, rasa-tattva, līlā-tattva āra*

**“Kṛṣṇa consciousness means understanding the truth of Kṛṣṇa, the truth of devotional service, the truth of love of Godhead, the truth of emotional ecstasy, the truth of transcendental mellows, and the truth of the pastimes of the Lord.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya*** **25.266)**

In simple terms, dear Prabhupāda,  
You are the embodiment of truth.  
In Kali-yuga, the fallen age,  
The light of knowledge shines through you.  
Deception, like a wild weed,  
Spreads its roots far and deep.  
It even sprouts in the overgrown gardens  
Where the *sādhus* sleep.  
Only the gardener knows the difference  
Between wild weed and creeper.  
And in the garden, who is awake  
And who the noisy sleeper?  
Illusion, deceit, ignorance—  
Aspects of our lingering pride.  
Deviousness, offense, and pretense  
In your presence can no longer hide.  
Your books, your example, and compassion,  
Your mood, instructions, and your passion (pure love),  
Distinguish nuances of truth  
From imitation and passing fashion.  
Your instructions are clear  
To those who chastely listen,  
And cleanse the heart  
Until, with *kṛṣṇa-prema,* it brightly glistens.  
On many roads one may stray  
Until the destination’s lost.  
The road signs of your instructions  
Keep our hearts focused.  
In truth you’ve given us Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa,  
Devotional service and Gaura *līlā,*  
Sweet nectar of the Holy Name,  
*Prasādam, pūjā, govardhana-śilā,*  
The truth of pure *samādhi,*  
Absorption in incessant preaching,  
The truth of Vaiṣṇava association—  
Even your departure, a perfect teaching.  
Our great fortune knows no bounds:  
We’ll always have your association.  
And with those who imbibe your mood  
Your *vāṇī* continues the purification.  
With hands folded and heads bowed  
We pray for your mercy, crystal-clear vision.  
Our love will practically manifest  
Through truth, through love, through cooperation .

Your eternal servants at New Goloka Dhām, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.

## New Hastinapura

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By your grace you remain present throughout the world, accepting our humble attempts to serve you. You are the giver of divinity, expanding the mercy of the six Gosvāmīs and other previous *ācāryas,* the Vedic literatures, Śrīmatī Tulasī-devī, and the Holy Name!

Please be merciful upon us. We pray that by following your instructions we will be blessed that your wishes will become our only goal in life. Please let us become the *mṛdaṅga* of Lord Caitanya, vibrating the Holy Name from door to door in every town and village.

As each day passes, I am more and more saddened and depressed by my shortcomings and lack of knowledge and devotion. Still, I am blessed with a small faith in the Holy Name, and so I try to grasp the Name firmly in my heart and vibrate it always on my tongue.

But I am fearful of the future. I am frightened by this world of birth and death. And if I achieve a little success in my spiritual life, then I quickly become captured by fame, honor, and power. Oh, please let me aspire to be a servant of the Vaiṣṇavas! Let me serve the Holy Name, let me understand that Guru and Gaurāṅga are all that I have and all that I need! Yes, daily, hourly, I see my inabilities!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the manifestation of Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda’s heart. I see so many capable persons serving you. All I want is to serve them somehow as they serve you. If I must remain in this cycle of birth and death, then let me accept repeated births as the humble servant of your servants.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for giving us yourself in the form of your books, your disciples, and this international society of devotees. Without these gifts our life would be of no value, being uselessly wasted in the frustrating pursuits of material existence. You are Lord Caitanya’s mercy personified. Following His example, you disregarded our lack of qualifications and gave us the highest happiness by teaching us how to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa and His devotees, distribute your books, chant Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Names, honor *kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* discuss and glorify Kṛṣṇa, and hold zestful festivals to share Kṛṣṇa with others.

Please give us your blessings and guidance so we can recommit our hearts and minds to serving your mission. Please let us stay united in our determination and dedication to your lotus feet. Let us work together cooperatively to bring glory to this movement. Let us always serve and glorify the Holy Name!

Your insignificant servants at New Hastināpura Dhāma, the ISKCON temple in Potomac, Maryland, USA.  
(written by Bhakta Steve)

## New Nandagram

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

You are a very dear associate of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and your glories are unlimited. All Vaiṣṇavas are glorified by their expansive transcendental glories, yet you stand out among the best of Vaiṣṇavas due to your unprecedented pastimes of compassion, by which you spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Your transcendental mood of compassion stands like a mountain that touches the sky and thus far surpasses the meritorious efforts of others, which appear like anthills in the shadow of you, the great giant among transcendentalists.

Standing as a testament to your supremely merciful mood are all your instructions, which are saturated with the burning desire to deliver all living beings back home to Godhead. Those who follow you always meditate upon this distinguishing feature of your teachings of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness philosophy and culture, because they have learned from you that the best way to please Kṛṣṇa is to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

You wrote, “Our principle should be that we are against nothing and for nothing. Only for Kṛṣṇa. We want whatever is favorable for Kṛṣṇa.” And “Our only motive is how to interest people in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” Therefore we meditate upon your mood and desire and remind ourselves that we are not to identify ourselves as cooks, managers, farmers, accountants, sweepers, *brahmacārīs,* or *gṛhasthas*, etc., but as the eternal servants of the servant of the servant of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and that our only business is to act in such a way that we attract everyone we meet back to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. This mood of devotional service helps spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It also creates a loving bond among all the members and departments of ISKCON and attracts the blessings of Śrī Guru and Śrī Kṛṣṇa, more so than any other mood of service.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, may you bless us with the preaching spirit.

All your servants at New Nandagram, Victoria, Australia.

## New Navadwipa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on the occasion of your appearance day!

*śuśrūṣoḥśraddadhānasya vāsudeva-kathā-ruciḥ*  
*syān mahat-sevayā viprāḥ puṇya-tīrtha-niṣevaṇāt*

**“O twice-born sages, by serving those devotees who are completely freed from all vice, great service is done. By such service, one gains affinity for hearing the messages of Vāsudeva.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.2.16)**

In your purport to this verse you write:

The servants of God come to propagate God consciousness, and intelligent people should cooperate with them in every respect. By serving the servant of God, one can please God more than by directly serving the Lord. The Lord is more pleased when He sees that His servants are properly respected because such servants risk everything for the service of the Lord and so are very dear to the Lord. The Lord declares in the *Bhagavad-gītā*(18.69) that no one is dearer to Him than one who risks everything to preach His glory. By serving the servants of the Lord, one gradually gets the quality of such servants, and thus one becomes qualified to hear the glories of God. The eagerness to hear about God is the first qualification of a devotee eligible for entering the kingdom of God.

So it is our great hope to serve you and try to please you. We are trying to chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* in humility and with great attention and sincere feeling. We are trying to preach the glorious message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu by various means. We are trying to develop a great desire to serve and please the Vaiṣṇavas. We are trying to serve you by carefully studying your transcendental books, which are filled with the most valuable instructions and your most intimate spiritual emotions.

So, we are trying to do our best just to have the great honor of being called Vaiṣṇavas and your sincere followers.

And, as usual, we need to ask you to pour your causeless mercy and blessings upon our heads. And what we really need right now is to learn how to cooperate with other devotees, how to appreciate others’ feelings toward Kṛṣṇa, and others’ sincere endeavors to please Guru and Kṛṣṇa. We need to learn to appreciate other devotees and help them increase their feelings for devotional service so that their service will become a spontaneous manifestation of their love and their free will to please Guru, Kṛṣṇa, and the Vaiṣṇavas. That is a great science and a great art—to inspire devotees to perform voluntary devotional service. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, mastered this great science, and therefore we ask you to please pour your blessings upon us so that we also will be able to perform this service in your mission.

Always trying to attain your causeless mercy,  
*Your servants in New Navadvīpa Mandir, Kiev, Ukraine.*

## New Nilācala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most humbles obeisances under the dust of your divine lotus feet, which are the abode of all surrendered devotees.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have countless qualities that are very difficult to describe on a sheet of paper in a few minutes. Indeed, even Lord Anantadeva could not describe all your extraordinary qualities. Therefore we shall describe just one of the qualities you showed in your dealings with all your disciples during the twelve years you lived with them. That quality is tolerance.

In the *Śrīmad-bhāgavatam* Lord Kapila Muni says to his mother, Devahūti, that a *sādhu* is *titikṣava* (tolerant), and in your purport you write that an example of this is that a devotee, “while engaged in preaching work, . . . has to meet with so many opposing elements.” When you came to the USA to start your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, you faced many problems, both with aspiring devotees and nondevotees, yet you tolerated their ignorance about your position as spiritual master.

When you began ISKCON in New York, you yourself cooked, served guests, and finally cleaned the temple and washed the pots. And when devotees lived with you, you sometimes had to wait to get into the shower in the morning. But you tolerated all these inconveniencies to establish your ISKCON. And even after you trained devotees how to render transcendental devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa, they often made many mistakes, but you tolerated them and instructed them again and again how to perform perfect loving devotional service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this is only a little description of the huge tolerance that you showed during your earthly pastimes, and we beg you to please give us strength to be more tolerant than a tree, as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu recommends in the third *śloka* of His *Śikṣāṣṭaka*.

Your most fallen, aspiring servants in New Nīlācala Mandir, Guadalajara, Mexico.  
(written by Premānanda dāsa)

## New Pānihāṭi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet on this most glories day of your divine appearance in this world, Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā.

It is by your divine mercy that we are able to remain in devotional service, and we pray that you will continuously bless us with the inspiration to serve you. The material energy is constantly trying to get us to deviate from the joyous path that you so masterfully laid out for us, but by remembering you we are able to fight back. When we think of your incredible struggle to establish this movement, we are immediately moved to take seriously our respective services. What you have done for us can never be repaid, not in a million lifetimes or even a billion lifetimes. We can only hope that our intelligence will always guide us to surrender to your will.

The material society is in great turmoil at the present moment, and therefore the time is ripe for this movement to penetrate the dense covering of *māyā* that keeps the population in such a state of confusion. With your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can make a major impact on the current situation by presenting your message. In fact, the entire world situation can be transformed by the spread of the *saṅkīrtana* movement and by making the world aware of your divine example and teachings. It is our duty to let the world know about you and what you have done for all mankind.

You appeared in this world not simply to start a religious movement and attract a few thousand followers. The reason why your spiritual master inspired you to travel westward was not just so that people would hear about the great Vedic culture from an accomplished Sanskrit scholar. Nor did you endure great hardships and personal frustrations so that a history of your endeavors would make interesting reading material. In reality you came to awaken, within all people, their dormant love of God known as Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You spread the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa to wipe away all their sorrows and inspire them to make devotional service the prime activity in their lives. And our only business should be to spend the valuable energy of our lives assisting you in this endeavor. In fact, we should arrange our lives in such a way that we have maximum time to devote to this most important work.

Of course, Śrīla Prabhupāda, without your mercy we cannot hope to ever achieve any appreciable level of success in spreading your mission worldwide. Our problem is that we sometimes think that by our mediocre intelligence we have done something significant. Actually, whatever we are allowed to do in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is simply due to your mercy in the form of your divine instructions, which are available in your books and other recorded works. Also, by hearing from your dear disciples who are absorbed in remembrance of you, we can have some idea how to proceed in taking your message to the masses. So these disciples of yours are also a manifestation of your mercy.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have truly blessed us in so many ways, and we pray that we can have the intelligence to take full advantage of your divine mercy. In the immortal words of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura:

*jogyatā-vicāre, kichu nāhi pāi, tomāra karuṇā-sāra*  
*karuṇā nā hoile, kāndiyā kāndiyā, prāṇa nā rākhibo āra*

**“If you examine me, you will find no good qualities. Your mercy is all that I am made of. If you are not merciful unto me, I can only weep, and I will not be able to maintain my life.” (“Gurudeva,” from** ***Śaraṇāgati,*** **verse 4)**

“Your mercy is all that I am made of” is such a wonderful meditation, given to us by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura as a way to think of the spiritual master. We should never think that we are independent of the mercy and blessings of the guru.

So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, keep us in a corner of your lotus feet and please allow us to always remain engaged in your service.

Your humble servants at New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, the ISKCON temple in Atlanta, Georgia, USA.  
(written by Balabhadra Bhaṭṭācārya dāsa)

## New Ramaṇ Reti

**What He Saw**

You found:

Material knowledge expanding; ignorance commanding.  
Riches and plenty; contentment not any.  
False fame to the small, worshiped by all.  
True greatness not found; silence in sound.  
For matter’s false forms, great burdens were borne.  
For spiritual upliftment there was no commitment.  
Darkness in lights, blindness in sights.  
The pathway was lost at incredible cost.  
On Kali’s new stage, old *saṁsāra* raged.  
The world was on fire with material desire.

**The Message**

Assessing our plight, you bathed us in light.  
Sweet summer rain, Hare Kṛṣṇa refrain.  
Your message was pure, your certainty sure.  
Not subject to time, beyond “me and mine.”  
The Absolute Truth, with logical proof.  
You pointed the way from night into day.  
Clouds pierced by the rays, the most ancient of ways,  
You gave us deliverance from darkness and ignorance.  
Scholars can’t find it though acting like pundits.  
Religion’s no better, self-righteousness shelter.  
Hopelessness gone, Caitanya’s new dawn.  
The Name’s our controller, our friend and consoler.  
The Name’s all around, the Deity in sound,  
Piercing the essence, revealing His presence.  
It’s bright and resplendent, on nothing dependent.  
It cleans out our hearts, real progress to start.

**The Order**

Now take this pure tonic, whether divine or demonic,  
Dissolving your pride, becoming your guide.  
A new life in time becoming sublime,  
Embracing the Name, not fortune and fame.  
Come to the place with nectarine taste.  
Bow as you enter, approach with surrender.

**The Destination**

This world’s not for those with hearts full of woes.  
Here there’s no sorrow, no fear of tomorrow.  
Serenity, calm, peaceful like dawn,  
It’s cool and refreshing, with loving compassion.  
Friendship, adventure, beauty, and splendor,  
Opulence shared, with joy unimpaired.  
His presence defines this pleasure divine,  
with consorts and friends—it’s life without end.

No one could have given more. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humbly thank you.

Your servants at New Ramaṇ Reti, ISKCON’s rural community in Alachua, Florida, USA.

## New Tālavan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace on your 108thappearance day! You are more glorified then glory itself, and we cannot understand enough to put into words even an infinitesimal drop of your infinite glory. Therefore, by your causeless mercy please accept our very feeble attempt to glorify you and celebrate this holiest of holy days, your appearance day.

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, another year has past and we languish in separation from you. At the time of this writing, one of our beloved Godbrothers, Śrīpād Śrīdhara Mahārāja, has just departed from this mortal world in the holy *dhāma* of Śrī Māyāpur. We have learned that at the final hour he was surrounded by devotees chanting the holy names, and he completed his worldly pastimes by lovingly gazing at your photo. He died prematurely, a victim of the deadly disease cancer.

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, during your manifest pastimes you always reminded us of our mortality. You told us about the temporary nature of our bodies. Often citing scriptural evidence, you proved to us that we are not our bodies. You showed us how to utilize our human form and return back to Godhead. Śrīdhara Swami knew the sublime truth very well, and as your deserving disciple and student he has returned home to you. Even though his body was taken by disease, his eternal self is with you and will serve you eternally. Such is the mercy of a *sad-guru* like yourself. Please give us the same blessings, so that at the end of this lifetime we too may return to you to serve you eternally. In our heart of hearts we feel that you have already given us that blessing, and now it is up to us to accept it and not stubbornly refuse to take it. Your mercy is truly causeless.

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, when you rolled up your manifested pastimes on this mortal planet, you left the charge of ISKCON, which is your body, to all your initiated disciples. All your disciples owe it to you to preserve and propagate your mission somehow or other.
