Even though all of us have not lived up to our full potential, we feel that, overall, ISKCON is moving in the right direction. Many wonderful things have happened in ISKCON worldwide this past year, too numerous to include in a short Vyāsa-pūjā homage. So let us give you an account of the best.

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva, the *yuga-avatāra* and His closest divine associates, have reappeared in Śrīdhām Māyāpur with all their glories. This has happened due to the loud calling from many of your devotees, who were seeking guidance for the future of ISKCON. After your departure, ISKCON had to overcome many stumbling blocks. Difficult to overcome were the growing pains of the movement that is destined to save the most fallen for the next 10,000 years. Because He heard our pleas, and for other confidential reasons, the most munificent Caitanya Mahāprabhu has reappeared in his Deity form, together with his eternal associates. His Deity form is not different from His own self.

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, many of your disciples, granddisciples, and countless other well-wishers have worked very hard to make this happen. You bestowed your blessings, and Caitanya Mahāprabhu has returned to take charge of the same movement He started more than five hundred years ago. The installation ceremony of Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva was so magnificent that the common consensus was that “ISKCON will never be the same again.”

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, you told us many times that it is Kṛṣṇa’s promise that his devotees will never be vanquished. Now we feel that neither the devotees nor your own body, ISKCON, can ever be vanquished. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted that in every town and village in this world His name would be sung. This prediction began to come true through your efforts, and now it’s time for us to rededicate ourselves to completing the process. In this sublime endeavor we feel sure that you are our ever well-wisher.

Your eternal servants at New Tālavan, ISKCON’s rural community in Mississippi, USA.

## New Vraja-dhāma (Hungary)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most blessed day!

You’re appearance day is always auspicious, but this year we celebrate the 108th anniversary of this holy event, so it’s extra auspicious. The more we remember and worship you, the more we get your mercy, the mercy of the pure devotee of the Lord.

*rahūgaṇaitat tapasā na yāti*  
*na cejyayā nirvapaṇād gṛhād vā*  
*na cchandasā naiva jalāgni-sūryair*  
*vinā mahat-pāda-rajo-'bhiṣekam*

**“My dear King Rahūgaṇa, unless one has the opportunity to smear his entire body with the dust of the lotus feet of great devotees, one cannot realize the Absolute Truth. One cannot realize the Absolute Truth simply by observing celibacy \[*****brahmacarya*****\], strictly following the rules and regulations of householder life, leaving home as a** ***vānaprastha,*** **accepting** ***sannyāsa,*** **or undergoing severe penances in winter by keeping oneself submerged in water or surrounding oneself in summer by fire and the scorching heat of the sun. There are many other processes to understand the Absolute Truth, but the Absolute Truth is only revealed to one who has attained the mercy of a great devotee.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **5.12.12)**

It is a plain fact that we would know nothing about the Absolute Truth if you hadn’t left holy Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma and gone to the West. There was no material reason for you to go to the West at the end of your sixties. People retire at that age. But you took the risk of traveling across the ocean to save us. Can we express our feelings with the mere words “Thank you” or “We are grateful”? Definitely not. You had the vision to save the whole world, so we cannot do anything else but take part in this transcendental mission and forget our personal motives—as you taught us by your personal example.

You established preaching centers, temples, Govinda’s restaurants, and farm communities all around the world, and many devotees came. Although the previous authorities did not let you enter Hungary in person, here there are now preaching centers, temples, a Govinda’s restaurant, and a farm community, New Vraja-dhāma. This is proof that you are still preaching through your books and your wonderful disciples. Is this not proof of your greatness? Your transcendental desires are taking shape in the form of these achievements, and also in the form of the devotees who are joining this movement to spread your fame in this heretofore unfortunate country.

Last year we celebrated the tenth anniversary of the founding of New Vraja-dhāma, and also the fact that 100,000 guests had visited Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara up to that time. Please cast your merciful glance upon us and bless us so that we can continue with our efforts to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Hungary and bring more and more people to the lotus feet of our beautiful Rādhe-Śyāma.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in New Vraja-dhāma, Hungary.  
(written by Śacīsuta dāsa)

## New Vrindaban

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In this world, the conditioned souls are at times racked with pain, numbed with fear, intoxicated with wealth, embittered by failure, blinded by success, disturbed by distress, overwhelmed with greed, and betrayed by their senses. We routinely become bewildered by life’s myriad pains and pleasures. The flickering happiness we obtain usually comes with great endeavor and at the expense of others. All the while, we remain unaware of death, which at any moment can rip us away from all we hold dear. We have wandered in this world sometimes as kings and sometimes as beggars. In our sojourn here we have experienced everything there is to experience, and still we remain unsatisfied.

O dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have appeared in this world to help us make a real solution to life’s twists and turns. Even though we are unqualified and undeserving, you have given us a chance to throw off the shackles of this material world. Nowhere in the history of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement has there been so much mercy bestowed upon the conditioned souls. Who can measure your kindness and compassion? Every moment of our lives should be filled with awe by your Herculean and divine passion to free us from illusion and unite us with the Supreme Lord, who is waiting patiently within our hearts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in New Vrindāvan we have an opportunity to serve you and Kṛṣṇa by serving the holy *dhāma* and by serving the pilgrims who come here. We have an opportunity to maintain this special place that you have created here in the Western world. We can only pray to Kṛṣṇa to allow us to understand, for even one moment, who you are and the great sacrifice you made for us. We pray to understand the depth of your teachings and the extent of your love for us. We pray to dwell at your lotus feet. We pray to understand that seeing your devotees and serving them is nondifferent from seeing and serving you personally. We pray to Lord Kṛṣṇa to let us be of some small service to you, for in this material world such an opportunity is very, very rare.

Your devotees in New Vrindāvan, West Virginia, USA.  
(written by Saṅkīrtana dāsa)

## New York (26 Second Avenue)

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace. All glories to your name, Śrīla Prabhupāda, which brings joy to so many disciples and granddisciples. All glories to your form, which adorns your ISKCON temples as well as the hearts of your followers worldwide. All glories to your pastimes, which lift the spirits of us all. We aspire to serve you in your pastimes now and always. We think that our greatest enjoyment comes from serving your ISKCON now, and after we leave our bodies we hope to serve the servants who give you the greatest pleasure.

*Yasya deve parā bhaktir yathā deve tathā gurau*. We cannot worship Kṛṣṇa without worshiping you, and we cannot worship you without worshiping Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Kindly allow me to offer you a status report on our service here at 26 Second Avenue, your first “Matchless Gifts” temple in the West. We have established a dynamic preaching center here, suitable for your holy *tīrtha*. *Janma karma ca me divyam evaṁ yo vetti tattvataḥ*. You once said that the *tīrtha* that has been established where the Lord performed His pastimes (*karma*) is more important than the *tīrtha* established where the Lord made his appearance (*janma*). Thus, we endeavor to preserve not only the place where you began your glorious activities in the West, but also the life and dynamism that you introduced there to our hardened and impure hearts.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, despite difficulties in your ISKCON, your many stalwart GBCs, gurus, and senior and junior men and women have remained enthusiastic, persevering, and tolerant. Here in the United States I am especially impressed by your agents Romapāda Swami, Mālatī Prabhu, Rādhānātha Swami, Anuttama Prabhu, Ravīndra Svarūpa Prabhu, Badrinārāyaṇ Prabhu, and Amarendra Prabhu, to name a few. They have taken on more than their share of the burdens of ISKCON. After your departure some of our Godbrothers irresponsibly tried to enjoy the fruits of ISKCON, putting great burdens on others. Yet the above-named disciples of yours have taken many of those burdens of ISKCON on their shoulders. They teach us all by their example. I pray to you, and all members of 26 Second Avenue pray to you, to allow us to share the glorious burden of service to you. Let us also be harassed by myriad problems relating to the maintenance and development of ISKCON.

Your great disciples who lead ISKCON seem to have the spiritual potency and strength of thousands of Jesus Christs and Mohammeds, although they are like candles to your sun. That you have so many wonderful disciples and followers is testament to your greatness and testament to the fact that you are empowering them even today.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we too often forget our identities as spirit souls, eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa. Thinking ourselves part of New York City, we become passionate like others here. Nevertheless, it is our impassioned plea to you to let us preach your glories along with the glories of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and the glories of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda. We anxiously, with *laulyam,* desire to distribute your books widely and cultivate the consciousness of Kṛṣṇa here in New York City.

Our fervent plea to you is that you implore Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Nityānanda-Balarāma to kindly empower us to distribute your mercy widely to the fallen souls of New York City.

Your followers at the ISKCON center at 26 Second Avenue, New York, New York, USA.  
(written by Dayānanda dāsa)

## New York (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*tasya tvaṁ tamaso ’ndhasya duṣpārasyādya pāragam*  
*sac-cakṣur janmanām ante labdhaṁ me tvad-anugrahāt*

**“Your Lordship is my only means of getting out of this darkest region of ignorance because You are my transcendental eye, which, by Your mercy only, I have attained after many, many births.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All sincere members of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement urgently need to study with unflinching faith your Bhaktivedanta purports of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in order to defeat the onslaughts of Māyā and make rapid progress on the path back home, back to Godhead. Your illuminating revelations, understanding, and love of Kṛṣṇa have a contagious purifying effect, capturing our hearts, opening our blind eyes, and enabling us to see the true light of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By this simple and effective method of *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ viṣṇoḥ smaraṇam* we keep our focus straight and true on the spiritual path leading to the ultimate goal, pure devotional service to Kṛṣṇa and *kṛṣṇa-prema*.

Any sincere soul blessed by an abundance of Kṛṣṇa consciousness realized through your teachings will be inspired to share the treasure of Kṛṣṇa consciousness by presenting *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Caitanya-caritāmṛta, The Nectar of Devotion,* etc., to many deserving unfortunate souls blinded and imprisoned by those two dominant and unforgiving prison-keepers, *tamo-guṇa* and *rajo-guṇa*. On this most glorious Vyāsa-pūjā day we pledge to dedicate ample time to reading your transcendental teachings and distributing your illuminating BBT publications, explaining the secret of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the true essence of Vedic wisdom, for the benefit of all aspiring devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

In all the ancient literatures of devotional service and in the more recent songs of Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura and other unalloyed Vaiṣṇavas, the spiritual master is always considered either one of the confidential associates of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī or a manifested representation of Śrīla Nityānanda Prabhu. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi* 1.47 purport\]

I perceive the core of ISKCON sevaites as dedicated and unique personalities, diverse yet sincerely striving for unity and cooperation in their effort to carry on your Kṛṣṇa consciousness mission. We are acutely aware of the many stumbling blocks on our path, challenges presented by Māyā in her attempt to derail the powerful ISKCON locomotive of love and devotion, and we are also acutely aware of your prudent warning mixed with your confident resolve: “The dogs may bark, but the caravan will pass.” Therefore I am sometimes led to think that ISKCON must be living and expanding on the mercy of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu. Lord Nityānanda is the most munificent incarnation of God, Lord Balarāma Himself. He is especially inclined to expand His mercy unto those unqualified because of misuse of independence, the most fallen, implicated in a life of sin. From the perspective of those who may be sober and wiser, these unique and sometimes very independent personalities would not be here except for that special unconditional mercy of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu. Therefore, at times, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have to admit to thinking you may be the empowered representative of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, the savior of the most fallen.

*jaya jaya śrī-caitanya jaya nityānanda*  
*jayādvaita-candra jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

**“All glories to Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu! All glories to Lord Nityānanda! All glories to Advaita Ācārya! And all glories to all the devotees of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu!”**

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, Advaita Prabhu, and Nityānanda Prabhu all belong to the *viṣṇu-tattva* category. Because Lord Caitanya is an ocean of mercy, He is addressed as *mahāprabhu,* whereas Nityānanda and Advaita, being two great personalities who assist Lord Caitanya, are addressed as *prabhu*. Thus there are two *prabhus* and one *mahāprabhu*. Gadādhara Gosvāmī is a representative of a perfect *brāhmaṇa* spiritual master. Śrīvāsa Ṭhākura represents a perfect *brāhmaṇa* devotee. These five are known as the Pañca-tattva. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi* 17.301 purport\]

This year thousands of fortunate devotees, your sincere servants, Śrīla Prabhupāda, had the most wonderful opportunity to witness and be part of—through *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanam* and Deity *sevā*the most glorious Pañca-tattva deity installation in Śrīdhām Māyāpur. We now understand more clearly the broad magnanimous potency of your transcendental ocean-of-bliss vision, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Just as Śrī Advaita Ācārya blessed mankind with His prayer that led to the descent and appearance of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, and the other members of the Pañca-tattva, so by your broad vision and pure potency of *kṛṣṇa-prema* you also commanded with love, arranging the descent and wonderful appearance of Śrī Gaurāṅga, Śrī Nityānanda Rāma, and the other members of Śrī Pañca-tattva. We see great hope now that Śrī Pañca-tattva have appeared in holy Māyāpur-dhāma for expanding Kṛṣṇa consciousness worldwide by the their almighty mercy of love and devotion. We pledge to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we are available to assist you in any menial way to deliver drops from that ocean of mercy and bliss now flowing from those beautiful lotus feet of Śrī Pañca-tattva in Māyāpur-dhāma. Please feel free to engage us as you see fit; we are your insignificant and unqualified menial servants. We feel very unqualified, and therefore we also have great hope that due to our lack of qualification we will especially attract the mercy of bliss and love that resides in the ocean of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu’s golden heart and in the golden heart of His pure devotee. Please don’t give up on us and kick us away from the soothing shelter of your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We pray to remain your menial servants birth after birth.

Your insignificant, unfortunate, and unqualified beggars at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandira, ISKCON’s temple in Brooklyn, New York, New York, USA.  
(written by Rāmabhadra dāsa)

## Ottawa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust at your lotus feet. All glories unto Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the anniversary of your glorious appearance day is always auspicious, but I believe this one, your one hundred and eighth, is extra special. This year, by your unlimited mercy, Their Lordships Śrī ŚrīPañca-tattva have re-appeared at Śrīdhām Māyāpur.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this is obviously the manifestation of your continuing compassion, kindness, mercy, and love for all of us, and indeed for all living entities on this planet. *Yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*. “By the mercy of the spiritual master, one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement.” Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are still doing everything to ensure that we have every opportunity to receive the benediction and mercy of the Lord and make some advancement toward becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us with sufficient intelligence that we faithfully adhere to the process and principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness given to us by Your Divine Grace. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said that a real disciple is one who follows the discipline given by the spiritual master. And if one does not follow the process given by the spiritual master, it will be as if one is trying to cook with smoke. The only result will be frustration: one will not achieve the desired result. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us to be consumed by the fire of pure devotional service at your lotus feet in our efforts toward achieving the ultimate goal of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in one of your lectures you showed the fallacy of the impersonalists’ philosophy—that the individual living entity is like a drop of water that falls from the clouds, then enters a stream, then floats down a river, and finally merges into the ocean—by saying that the sun again evaporates the water and it again forms clouds and again falls as rain. You further stated that there are many varieties of aquatics in the ocean, and the bigger ones, like the shark, go deep into the waters and are therefore not evaporated. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us dive deeper and deeper into the nectarean ocean of pure devotional service unto you. This will protect us from being evaporated by the heat of the forest fire of material existence or being devoured by the different crocodilelike philosophies that are circling the boat of your ISKCON.

Śrī Prabhupāda, you are the most qualified captain of the ISKCON boat. Please help us always execute our service on the ISKCON boat, with your blessings, under your guidance, and for your pleasure. Your personal purity and the purity of the message you gave us is certainly the force that attracted us, and it continues to keep us safely at your lotus feet. Help us so that we may also become worthy of the causeless mercy you have bestowed upon us, and allow us to assist you in your mission to save as many living entities as possible by purely presenting the pure science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you so kindly presented to us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humble pray that by your mercy, in the association of your devotees, we may develop some true taste for service at your lotus feet, beginning with a taste for hearing and chanting your glories and the glories of the Lord and His Holy Names, form, qualities, pastimes, etc. We pray to develop a drop of your compassion and love for others. We also pray that, out of love for you, we may cooperatively strive to please you by remaining as specks of dust at your lotus feet and thus becoming suitable instruments to give others the good fortune of taking shelter at the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace. All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your eternally grateful servants at the ISKCON temple in Ottawa, Canada.  
(written by Śaṅkara dāsa)

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to your appearance day! You are the one who came here from Kṛṣṇaloka and received the flame of the *mahā-mantra* from your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You performed the biggest *saṅkīrtana-yajña* marathon, traveling from India to the USA, and then traveled around the world to sanctify the planet. By multiplying this flame of Lord Caitanya, you offered the Holy Name to everyone who was willing to accept it. Your desire was that your disciples take up the same flame and run for the Olympic Games of *harināma*. From the core of your truly pure heart, you wished that every soul on earth could hear the name of Kṛṣṇa, and that not a single soul would remain lost in the darkness.

The instructions of your Guru Mahārāja are always shining like the flames of wood embers in your soul. You will be eternally cherished within the history of Vaiṣṇavism as he who miraculously crossed the world at the age of seventy, with hardly any money, to enable thousands of people to chant the *mahā-mantra,* just like Lord Caitanya in the Jhārikhaṇḍa Forest. This miraculous deed was due to your immense goodness and your great love for your guru and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara. You are the most beautiful person. Whoever saw you, heard from you, associated with you, or listened to your pastimes was saved from the cycle of repeated birth and death.

All the universes chant your glories. Thank you very much for never letting us forget you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We still have a very strong desire: Please let us go wherever you are with all our Godbrothers and -sisters when we leave this body. Let us continue *harināma-saṅkīrtana* and help you in your service to Kṛṣṇa. The *śāstras* say we should relate to Kṛṣṇa through the guru and offer him the most humble obeisances, but today we directly beg Lord Kṛṣṇa to listen to our prayer: “We wish to take birth again as an insignificant servant of our spiritual master and guide, Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who came to save us in this present life.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we cannot describe all your glories. We are your most fallen servants and always feel separation from you. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are always at your command, and we beg you to accept us as your servants, birth after birth.

Your servants in the ISKCON temple in Paris, France.  
(written by Kiśorī-devī dāsī)

## Pecs

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your appearance in the material world is the greatest benediction for all living entities. Having appeared in this world of conditioned souls, you taught us how to eradicate the blazing fire of material existence for good and how to escape from the cycle of birth and death.

You have made it possible for sinful souls to realize their original identity and awaken their dormant love of God. Ordinary people cannot show the way leading to God, but you, being the most confidential servant of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, crossed the ocean at the age of seventy, risking your life. In your heart you carried the instruction of your spiritual master to spread the mission of Lord Caitanya all over the world.

Upon seeing the godless civilization of the Western world, you prayed to the Lord with deep compassion and perfect humility:

But I guess You have some business here, otherwise why would You bring me to this terrible place? . . . How will they understand the mellows of devotional service? O Lord, I am simply praying for your mercy so that I will be able to convince them about Your message.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, yes, you were able to convey the message of the Lord because you were a perfect instrument in the hands of your beloved spiritual master. You used all your time and energy to preach, to print books, and to present the Vedic culture. You gave us the philosophy of simple living and high thinking. You chanted Kṛṣṇa’s name with unflinching faith, and you rendered more service to Kṛṣṇa’s name than anybody else because you took it to each and every town and village on earth.

Your one-person enthusiasm has developed into a worldwide movement, a huge society of Vaiṣṇavas. Farm communities and temples are being built all around the world.

When they read your books, tens of thousands of people change their views of life. As a result of your matchless endeavors, Kṛṣṇa consciousness has been flourishing in Hungary, too, for more than fifteen years. Your perfectly faithful disciples, incessantly serving your lotus feet, have manifested the glorious New Vraja Dhām, where devotees are engaged in the devotional service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara. Śrī Śrī Dayāl Nitāi Vijay Gaurāṅga have arrived in a wonderful preaching mood, and Their mercy is limitless: they are chanting the holy name of Lord Hari to save the fallen souls in the Age of Kali.

The generals and soldiers of Lord Caitanya have personally brought us your transcendental writings, which open our eyes, blinded by the darkness of ignorance.

Thank you for the strength you give us daily so that we can present your books to the people. Thank you for the Christmas marathons, which put us through trials but at the end of which we can always place wonderful experiences deep into our hearts and keep them there.

We pray to you a lot so that you may be pleased with us and allow us to go on preaching and distributing books, which please you most.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda: our greatest treasure, by your mercy, is that we may serve and associate with holy Vaiṣṇavas. Please give us your mercy so that we may become fixed in devotional life, and please help us always remember our spiritual master’s words: that we are only tiny and insignificant servants of yours.

Your servants in Pecs, Hungary.  
(written by Śyāma-Pyāri-devī dāsī and Govinda-Priyā-devī dāsī)

## Perth

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Unlimited Mercy**

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are our savior.  
You have saved us  
From quickly gliding down to the  
Hellish planets.  
You have given us Kṛṣṇa  
In many forms—  
The Holy Name,  
The Deity,  
Spiritual foodstuffs,  
*Śāstra,*  
And the Vaiṣṇavas.  
Please allow us to take  
Full advantage  
Of your unlimited  
*mercy*.  
You stand among  
Other luminaries,  
Such as Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī  
And Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura,  
As someone whose compassion  
Was purely spiritual.  
Not just saving bodies  
But saving  
Souls.  
You said in 1975 (Denver):  
“They are taking this  
Temporary life as everything.  
Very horrible condition.  
We are trying to explain  
What is actual life.”  
Please allow us to  
Appreciate your unlimited  
*compassion*.  
It wasn’t easy  
In the beginning—  
To establish a Society—  
And as ISKCON grew  
It needed a lot of your  
Guidance.  
But you never  
Wavered in the wind.  
You stood firm  
With Kṛṣṇa and Guru  
By your side.  
Please allow us to  
Have even a drop  
Of your unlimited  
*determination.*

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. By you mercy we will try to do our part to help carry on your mission.  
*Your Servants in ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi temple in Perth, Australia.*

## Philadelphia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your appearance in this world has forever changed the prognosis for the Age of Kali. We were all destined to slide deeper under the spell of the material energy. Propelled by our own lust, anger, and greed, and invited by the allure of the machines of Māyā, we were dancing into the fires of hell, thinking ourselves happy and on the path of progress.

Your Divine Grace has shed light upon our dark minds and given hope for reality, clarity, beauty, and truth. Your Divine Grace has pierced the armor of steel around our hearts to plant the seeds of *bhakti*. Our hearts are no longer vacant; rather, they are filled with the warmth of Kṛṣṇa’s kindness and the joy of His sweetness.

How does one repay you, who have given us everything of value? We can only follow your example with devotion, taking care not to sully your clear and perfect instructions with our own irreverence and cheating need.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, forgive us our mistakes and offenses. Save us from false philosophies. Give us the intelligence and sincerity to follow you faithfully. Protect us from a lack of gratitude.

Give us your blessings so that we may become Kṛṣṇa conscious and assist you in giving Kṛṣṇa to everyone.

Your humble servants in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.  
(written by Saudāmanī-devī dāsī)

## Phoenix (South Africa)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha! All glories to ŚrīPañca-tattva! And all glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this most auspicious day of your 108th Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, we would like to offer our eternal gratitude to you for allowing us to become part of your glorious family and be able to serve your devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything that one could hope for in this material world. You have created the spiritual realm within this material world. By the spiritual potency of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Nityānanda Prabhu, you have also shown us how this material world is a place of suffering, of repeated birth and death. Your ISKCON society is the spiritual world within this material world, and any involvement outside of ISKCON is fraught with danger.

We have seen how many devotees have left ISKCON and started their own societies, but these societies do not have the same spiritual potency ISKCON has. You have stated many times that this ISKCON society is the main branch of the Caitanya tree. You are undoubtedly the most confidential servitor of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, a fact proven by the success and spiritual potency of your ISKCON.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am most grateful for the opportunity to render some service to your ISKCON and I sincerely pray that you please be merciful to me by keeping me engaged in service in your ISKCON. I did not have the honor of meeting you personally, but due to the association of your stalwart disciples and my spiritual master, I feel a strong connection to you. My desire is to always try to please you by serving my spiritual master and the devotees of your ISKCON, because I know that by serving these sincere and dedicated followers of yours, you will be pleased, and that will be the perfection of this fallen soul.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful to this unfortunate soul and allow me to show my gratitude not only by writing words but by doing something practical to enhance the glory of Your Divine Grace.

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Phoenix, South Africa.  
(written by Prabhānu dāsa)

## Porto Alegre

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and respectful obeisances onto your lotus feet. Due to your unmotivated mercy, we all fortunately have the opportunity to take part in this marvelous and most blessed event, your Vyāsa-pūjā ceremony.

There is no doubt that if it weren’t for you we would be acting like cats and dogs, constantly searching for sense gratification and bereft of any meaningful purpose in life. But thanks to your blessings, we have come to know about devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

You saved millions of fallen souls who hardly had any awareness and elevated them to their original Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And due to your grace, this is still happening. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are all very grateful and eternally indebted. There is only one way to repay you for even a fraction of this invaluable benediction: to eternally offer our service to the Lord in this marvelous society you’ve created called ISKCON. We must continually endeavor to expand ISKCON so that more and more people in need can purify themselves.

You have extended your causeless mercy through your books and disciples. ISKCON is a priceless treasure, which you, our Mahā-grandfather, has left to us grandchildren and to future generations. As your grandchildren, we have inherited the most valuable treasure: a big house with an even bigger family. Therefore, it is our responsibility to esteem and care for this asset.

Your eternal servants in Porto Alegre, Brazil.  
(written by Mathurā-nātha dāsa)

## Pretoria

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Compassion**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, upon reflecting on your most wonderful and glorious appearance, we find there are aspects of your divine personality that get us to conclude that you are one of those spiritual personalities of our time that had compassion of a depth that is inconceivable to mere mortals of this world.

How do we get to know you, Śrīla Prabhupāda? You answered this by saying “If you want to know me, read my books.” We are reading, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and want to make sure that we are able to read every word that you wrote or spoke. We know that you also stated that you spoke directly to Śrī Kṛṣṇa and that those “conversations” are shared with us through your books. We know now that as we advance on this most wonderful path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness your books are bringing us closer to knowing Kṛṣṇa through the guidance of your pure representatives.

Our appreciation for your purports, which you described as your “emotional ecstasies,” is growing more and more, and this is evidenced by our appreciative discussions of these with devotees. Your books, and especially your purports, are transporting us to wonderful levels of ecstasy, and we are getting a wonderful taste and enthusiasm at our youthful center and in our early devotional life.

You stated clearly: “So, there is nothing to be said new. Whatever I have to speak, I have spoken in my books.” We take this seriously, and it is clear that whatever is needed for us to progress in spiritual life is to be found in your books. Your books will certainly be “the law books of human society for the next ten thousand years.” We see a lot of dedicated students of your books, and it gives us comfort to see the positive change that these books are making in devotees. We have a small preaching center in downtown Pretoria, the capital of South Africa, and our main focus is preaching through the distribution of your books. Please bless us that we can continue this wonderful service and that we can do it better and grow stronger.

The compassion you displayed when dealing with your disciples is truly amazing, and in listening to them speak about you, we are left with renewed hope about our journey back to Godhead. For not only have you transferred your compassionate nature to your disciples, but most importantly you have shown your compassion for all humankind by the books you so expertly wrote. These books of yours are the tools and roadmaps that you left us to ensure that we all go back home to the spiritual sky. You created ISKCON as your body, as the vehicle through which we can worship Kṛṣṇa and associate with Vaiṣṇavas; in this way we may live the instructions in your books so that we all can make spiritual advancement on the path back to Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Our dear beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this, your most auspicious Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, we dedicate ourselves to you and your ISKCON. Please bless us so that we can be of use to you and your pure disciples in ISKCON, so that we can render some meaningful service and develop a fraction of a drop of your compassion.

His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

The most unworthy servants of your servants at the ISKCON temple in Pretoria, South Africa.

## Pune

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in this dark age of misleading education, you illuminated the entire planet with the transcendental torchlight of *rāja-vidyā,* supreme education in the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You desired that educated and intelligent youths be taught the glory of Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that they may become future beacon-lights for the whole world. In one of your empowered and empowering letters you gave the vision and the foundation for college preaching:

\[T\]ry to recruit some of the intelligent class of student class to take up this Krsna Consciousness philosophy and study it carefully. Try to recruit some of such men to become our devotees. There is need for intelligent young men to train them up as future leaders and preachers to go all over the world for spreading the message of Lord Caitanya Mahaprabhu. If we simply go to the college on one night and leave immediately for another place, it will be difficult to make very much impression upon these students. I think it is better to stay at one place for some time, at least one week, or as long as the students are interested, and try to sell as many books as possible. After your lecture in the college you may request if there are any interested students, that they may make further programs for you in their homes, or in some club or hall, like that, so that you may remain some time longer and concentrate on recruiting two or three good men, whoever appears to be the most interested, and by your diligent preaching to them you may be able to persuade some of them to become our devotees.

We, your servants in Pune, which is known by many as the “Oxford of the East,” have been inspired, in addition to carrying on the regular temple programs, to focus on spreading your mercy among the college youth. To implement your vision as outlined in the above letter, we have attempted to develop specialized youth preaching centers called BACE (Bhaktivedanta Academy for Culture and Education), which aim to give a solid spiritual base to the lives of youths and to society at large. These preaching centers are apartments located strategically very close to major colleges so that spiritually inclined students can easily come for the programs and serious devotee-students can also reside together. Each center is like a mini-ISKCON temple, complete with altar, morning program, kitchen, *prasādam* distribution, book distribution, reading room, well-equipped library of books, audio- and videotapes, and, most important, vigorous preaching. The preachers from the temple come several times a week to conduct preaching programs, and the devotee students residing in the center call their co-students for the program. The extension center thus serves as both a safe shelter for the devotee students and a strategic outpost for the youth preachers.

By your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in just seven years the nine BACEs all over India—five near the major colleges of Pune and the other three adjacent to the Indian Institute of Technology in Kharagpur, the Benares Hindu University in Vararansi, the Indian Institute of Information Technology in Gwalior, and the National Institute of Technology in Warangal—have brought Kṛṣṇa into the lives of thousands of students. Several hundred engineering students have started practicing spiritual life seriously. Over one hundred twenty-five student-leaders, all chanting sixteen rounds, are receiving systematic philosophical training through specially designed courses based on your books. And most importantly, forty young graduates, seven of them with master’s degrees and twenty-three of them engineers, have dedicated their lives to spreading far and wide the message you brought for the world.

Indeed, by your inconceivable mercy the response we have received is so overwhelming that at present we are unable, at our present temple, to provide good residential and other facilities for the youths coming to take shelter of your lotus feet. We hope that we will be able to serve them better in a new temple, which we pray will come up in the near future. On this auspicious day of your 108th Vyāsa-pūjā, we seek your blessings and mercy so that we may be able to continue and expand our humble services to your lotus feet.

Your servants at ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī Mandir in Pune, India.

## Rāḍhadesh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

In the darkness and cold of the early morning, in a courtyard far away from the chaos of the city, figures draped in flowing garments are running from all directions. Leaving their homes for the castle, with fixed attention, each soul is absorbed in reaching the destination of Rādhā-Gopīnātha’s lotus feet. This is early morning at Rāḍhadesh.

From where is such determination coming? Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is coming from you. Sincere souls, from all corners of the world, are now assembled here. They have reached the place where they can run to the lotus feet of Rādhā-Gopīnātha without hindrance, due to your grace.

Life in Rāḍhadesh is very sweet when Kṛṣṇa is in the center.

*veṇur madhuro reṇur madhuraḥ*  
*pāṇir madhuraḥ pādau madhurau*  
*nṛtyaṁ madhuraṁ sakhyaṁ madhuraṁ*  
*madhurādhi-pater akhilaṁ madhuraṁ*

**“His flute is sweet, His foot-dust is sweet,**  
**His hands are sweet, His feet are sweet,**  
**His dancing is sweet, His friendship is sweet—**  
**Everything is sweet about the Emperor of Sweetness.”**

Deity greeting is a festival that, for some, continues throughout the day. There is a special attention, a special kind of greed, in the hearts of the Rāḍhadesh-vāsīs: to see Rādhā-Gopīnātha. In what beautiful style and colors will They appear today? This sweetness was born in our lives by our connection to you, and such sweetness increases in a grateful heart. Just as the *pūjārīs* reveal the Deity by their devotion, you reveal to us the sweetness of Kṛṣṇa by your devotion. We would like to offer you our grateful hearts, and we can show our gratitude to you by carefully following your instructions. Please accept our attempts to do so.

The residents of Rāḍhadesh-dhāma are finding unity in diversity. We are a small village of many different individuals: leaders and managers, cooks and cleaners, scholars and students, *pūjārīs* and gardeners. The starting points of our lives and our vocations may be different, but we are unified by what you have given us. Our lives are made sacred by what you have given us.

Your devotees in Rāḍhadesh welcome tens of thousands of visitors each year, introducing each to Kṛṣṇa. We pray this will continue and flourish, because these are not just ordinary tourists. Through your teachings we receive the eyes to recognize that each soul coming here has been specially granted this opportunity to come toward Gopīnātha. We can see that each guest is His friend and that we are His servants. *Īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ hṛd-deśe ’rjuna tiṣṭhati.* The Supreme Lord is situated in everyone’s heart. Let us see the beauty in each situation in our lives, in each person in our lives, and welcome each friend, running or walking, toward the lotus feet of Gopīnātha. Please let us be your instruments to reveal His sweetness and glory to them.

The fortunate forest flowers of Rāḍhadesh give happiness to each person passing by, as they fully open to the sun, who sustains them and who gives them life. As we are passing through this life, let the rays of your grace, through your teachings and your surrendered followers, be our life and soul, and may we always run toward the service of Your Divine Grace with as much eagerness as in the early morning hours.

Your servants at Rāḍhadesh, Belgium.

## Recife

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Very, very dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, when we celebrate the 108th anniversary of your appearance, the congregational devotees from the ŚrīPañca-tattva temple of Recife are here to glorify you 108 years after planet Earth become full of joy due to your arrival.

It is said that an astrologer predicted you would cross the ocean and found 108 temples all around the world. It is also said that, some days after you left this world in 1977, the Recife temple was founded due to the efforts of some pioneers led by your disciple Jagad Vicitra dāsa Prabhu, following the guidelines given by His Holiness Hridayānanda dāsa Goswami.

It is considered that Recife is the 108th ISKCON temple. The community that nowadays renders devotional service at this outpost of your divine mission feels obliged to honor and move forward the missionary efforts of the founders. We also strive to find the means that the present time demands from the Prabhupādānugas, so that we can adequately conduct preaching programs in a consistent way, conforming to your legacy. We feel honored to be part of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, trying in our humble way to help these very productive and pious people, in whose hearts Kṛṣṇa consciousness has found fertile soil. Indeed, the seed of *bhakti* that you, despite being so distant, sowed via your disciples’ hands is growing nicely.

In this way, on this 108th anniversary of your appearance, Śrīla Prabhupāda, each and every devotee who makes the Recife temple possible prays to remain a humble servant in your mission. We glorify you 108 times and ask you to bless the *sannyāsīs* and all the other leaders of the Brazil *yātrā* so that they, being protected and inspired by you, may also protect and inspire us to continue chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Sincerely,  
Your servants at ISKCON’s ŚrīPañca-tattva Mandir in Recife, Brazil.

## Rio de Janeiro

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

ISKCON is the true manifestation of your unmotivated mercy. You dedicated all your life to satisfying the desire of your spiritual master, and simultaneously you fulfilled Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s prediction. You also kindly taught us to develop proper consciousness and granted us protection through this institution, which, although acting in this material existence, is situated entirely in the spiritual realm.

Therefore, all those who sincerely stay in touch with your ISKCON and follow your teachings and personal example can achieve the highest goal of life: loving service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa states, “Those who try to serve Me directly aren’t My true devotees, but those who serve My devotees are very dear to Me.” We have been given this rare opportunity to engage in the service of the Lord’s pure devotee—you, Śrīla Prabhupāda—and this rare opportunity is available to us only because of your intense desire to indiscriminately save all fallen souls.

You have taught us everything, from elementary human behavior up to the most sublime aspects of the Absolute Truth, such as those displayed in the supreme *līlā* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us remain under the protection of your lotus feet; otherwise, any spiritual endeavor in our lives is meaningless.

Your servants in the ISKCON temple in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil.  
(written by Mahāprabhu-kṛpa dāsa)

## Sacramento

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness, on the most auspicious occasion of his appearance day!

Approximately five thousand years have passed since the beginning of Kali-yuga, when the human race began its downward descent into the dark regions of a world devoid of knowledge of its own purpose or origins and without hope of any light and guidance as to the meaning of existence and the path to everlasting life. Since that ominous moment in the interminable revolutions of the cosmic machinery, humanity has been struggling with ever-increasing untold miseries and oppression from all sides, afflicted by the cruelty of tyrants, varieties of narrow-minded, self-serving priesthoods, poverty, famine, disease, and war.

All of this suffering and turmoil was suddenly dissipated by the wonderful and magnificent appearance of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and his eternal associates, who blessed the world with the chanting the Holy Names of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Of all the great *ācāryas* who have followed in the wake of Lord Caitanya, each one increasing the ocean of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa, none has excelled in expanding the mission of spreading pure love God worldwide more than His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. In twelve short years he established the International Society for Krishna Consciousness on all the continents of the world, published translations of the major scriptures of *bhāgavata-dharma* in many languages, and amassed a following of many thousands of initiated disciples and admirers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s achievement is in reality a seed that, once sown by His Divine Grace, is to be watered and cared for with great attention by his followers so that it can actually fructify and achieve the purpose for which it was given, namely, to save the whole world from the twin disasters of impersonal materialism and self-annihilation. Within this grand scheme there is potentially a plan of salvation for each and every soul in this world, because each one of them belongs to Kṛṣṇa, even if it appears that they are presently sunk deep in various shades of ignorance. In this lifetime each soul’s ability to access this amazing opportunity to go home, back to Godhead, will depend upon his response to the invitation given by Kṛṣṇa’s devotees to chant the Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa and join the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya. The gradual unfolding of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mission to liberate the whole world is therefore a symbol of the advent of the Golden Age of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and Śrīla Prabhupāda’s instructions and guidance will carry humanity forward, with great authority, into this Golden Age, when all the symptoms of a worldwide civilization based on the principles of *varṇāśrama* and devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa through the chanting of His Holy Names will be manifest.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your humble servants in Sacramento, California, are working hard to fulfill your vision and are praying for your kindness and blessings to fall upon us so we may be succeed in our attempt to establish your mission on a firm footing in this tiny insignificant corner of the universe, and with one heart and one voice we declare, “All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, Jagad-guru, savior of the whole world!”

*Your servants in Sacramento, California, USA.*

## Salvador

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet.

How can we better express our feelings on this very special day of yours than by saying “Thank you very much!” Today we commemorate the 108th anniversary of your appearance on this planet, and this event has certainly changed our lives. Many conditioned souls who dwell in this material existence are collecting the benefits of your appearance even while unaware of your existence. Yes, due to your great influence, all conditioned souls in the material world are benefited, even those living in other universes—an effect of something we might term synergy.

But whatever may be the correct term, it is a fact that your influence is felt in all parts of the creation. We personally experience your powerful influence even twenty-seven years after your departure from this world. We constantly witness many lives being transformed by your words, example, and love. You have performed a task that no one has in the past, and definitely no one every will in the future.

You gave us the matchless gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. One may think you are replaceable, but that is a great mistake, for you performed a unique task: accomplishing your spiritual master’s mission even amidst immense difficulties and obstacles, such as lack of support, deep ignorance, low education, poor hygiene, egocentric or childish behavior, drugs, and liberal sex. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you tolerated what anyone would consider madness. You had your personal belongings stolen, were hassled by LSD users, were homeless, and lacked any assistance from India.

You had every reasonable motive to put aside the mission and return to Vṛndāvana, rest in ease, and conclude that the mission was over because all necessary measures were taken and efforts exhausted. But instead, your determination remained unaffected. Your love toward your spiritual master and his instructions was so great that your faith remained unflinching.

Oh! What would have become of us if you had given up! Oh! What would our lives have been! You saved us from unthinkable degradation. For this reason there isn’t much we can say on this very special day of your appearance other than “Thank you very much!”Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for having so much will power, determination, patience, humility, and love for us fallen souls.

Your eternal servants in the ISKCON temple in Salvador, Brazil.  
(written by Aniruddha dāsa)

## Sao Paulo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine ISKCON!

On this glorious anniversary of your appearance in this world, our happiness and rejoicing have no limit. You changed world history, bringing the causeless mercy of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, whose teachings and compassion can break materialistic logic and then rewrite human destiny.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternally grateful and indebted to your divine lotus feet. You saved us from ignorance and are giving us the privilege to participate in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We understand that all living beings are benefiting from your sublime movement.

You gave us the perfect formula for saving humanity and gave the responsibility for organizing this mission to your disciples. Inspired by Your Divine Grace, we are trying to assist them in pushing on this transcendental movement, preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness to conditioned people.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in the Sao Paulo Mandir we are trying to serve together to fulfill your sublime mission. We pray at your lotus feet for the blessings to serve with love and trust so we can be effective in helping people taste the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

All glories to the *paramahaṁsa* of *paramahaṁsas,* Śrī Śrīmad A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

Your servants in Sao Paulo, Brazil.  
(written by Prāṇa Nātha dāsa)

## Seattle

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Before your ever-fresh presence we reverently prostrate our bodies and minds in eternal celebration of your causeless love for countless conditioned souls throughout the seven continents. You personally emanated the golden rays of Lord Caitanya’s *kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana,* tirelessly inducing hope in the profoundly downtrodden simply by your indescribable glance of compassion and empathy. The reverberating trumpet call issued through your spoken and written discourses continually vibrates with an intensity that rivals the light of the summer sun.

All your conscious efforts were focused upon fulfilling the heartfelt transcendental wishes of your eternal well-wisher, His Divine Grace Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī GosvāmīṬhākura. With inscrutable humility you kindly shared that inestimable mission across half a world with us spiritually uneducated floundering aspirants of the devotional path. Selflessly you deposited into our eager uncultured hearts the *bhakti-latā-bīja*.

With the infinite patience of a loving father you expertly began to engage childlike Western students in the greatest worldwide outreach effort ever, the effort to disseminate the glorious *līlās* of Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Simultaneous with the training and management of neophyte devotees and the inauguration of temples worldwide, you assiduously translated the essence of Vedic wisdom from Sanskrit and Bengali into English. This merciful translation work consumed your dedication until almost your last breath.

Your sweetness, meticulous concern, and all-encompassing patience enacted through the visible pastimes with your dedicated men and women disciples knew no practical limits. Unto your lotus feet we fall like bamboo saplings, begging to be utilized as minute elements in the ISKCON mercy ship.

We witness today the incredible *śakti* of your selfless footsteps throughout the cities and rural areas in both prominent and most obscure countries. Today your disciples and granddisciples struggle valiantly, inspired by the dynamic memory of your example to engage the materialistic and impersonalistic nature of this chaotic age. The living spirit of your pristine example thrives through innumerable accomplishments. Devotees within and without the ISKCON family emulate your preaching strategy and style. But all recognize with profuse admiration that it is you who have opened the contemporary gates of Western culture to the far-reaching effects of Vaiṣṇava philosophers and *sannyāsī* mendicants who now so diligently present the causeless mercy of Caitanyadeva to the forlorn souls of the world. It is you, our most dear friend, Śrīla Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who eternally extend beyond time and form into the hearts and minds of present generations, igniting the spark of *bhakti* to the Supreme ŚrīŚrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants in Seattle, Washington, USA.  
(written by Dāmodara Paṇḍita dāsa)

## Secundrabad

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

How simply you presented the most sublime knowledge! Even great thinkers, yogis, and mystics couldn’t find the ultimate meaning of the *Vedas*. Then you came to the kingdom of the *mlecchas* and cleared the path for all to go back home, back to Godhead. Simple and sublime is your message, and simple and sublime your process to receive it.

As we look around the world today, we see that people are very unhappy, being severely afflicted by the threefold miseries. We see a fast-degrading culture full of crime, suicide, mass killings, and many other indescribably heinous acts being committed, from the big cities to the most remote villages. People everywhere are in anxiety.

But then we open your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and read: “The bull is the emblem of the moral principle, and the cow is the representative of the earth. When the bull and the cow are in a joyful mood, it is to be understood that the people of the world are also in a joyful mood.” These words are so relishable. The solution is so simple. But just a few days back we saw cows being taken very mercilessly to the slaughterhouse in the same land where cows are supposed to be worshiped. It seems the demons will never stop tampering with the material energy until the entire creation is destroyed. They complicate everything and go to great lengths to avoid the simple laws of God: Just protect the cow and bull, become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and be joyful! Therefore if we read your books and follow your instructions given in them, thus gaining the potency to distribute them to others, we will without a doubt become Kṛṣṇa conscious within this lifetime and go back home, back to Godhead, dragging thousands of conditioned souls along with us in the process. All this we may accomplish only by your mercy.

Also, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we see that in this age the training that children and youth throughout the world are receiving is training befitting animals. The youth are sent off to schools and colleges (“slaughterhouses,” you termed them), where nonsense is stuck in their minds and where teachers are teaching that God doesn’t exist and that the goal of life is to enjoy wildly by gratifying one’s senses. When frustration occurs, the young people resort to bizarre activities—even suicide. In this way the future generations are being trained to lean toward a godless civilization. The only hope is if the younger generation can be trained in Kṛṣṇa consciousness by the simple way you showed; then this world will be saved from its destructive course.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as your granddisciples, we are dutybound to work for the establishment of the “other half” of your work—the establishment of *varṇāśrama*—although we feel quite inadequate to comprehend the magnitude and gravity of your vision. Therefore we are praying fervently on this auspicious day of your divine appearance for a dispensation of special mercy from your lotus feet.

The insignificant servants of your servants in Secunderabad, India.

## Silicon Valley

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer you our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the literary incarnation of Godhead, is very merciful to the conditioned souls. Hoping to alleviate the miseries of all the poor souls suffering in Kali-yuga and to bring them good fortune, he made arrangements to widely distribute the messages of Vāsudeva. He divided the *Atharva Veda* into four so that it could be more widely circulated. He then compiled the *Purāṇas* and presented the *Mahābhārata* to attract the interest of the dull population of Kali-yuga. Finally, after receiving the instruction of Nārada Muni, Vedavyāsa revealed the cream of all the Vedic literatures, the spotless *Purāṇa, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Sūta Gosvāmī exalts these magnificent acts of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, saying:

*anarthopaśamaṁ sākṣād bhakti-yogam adhokṣaje*  
*lokasyājānato vidvāṁś cakre sātvata-saṁhitām*

**“The material miseries of the living entity, which are superfluous to him, can be directly mitigated by the linking process of devotional service. But the mass of people do not know this, and therefore the learned Vyāsadeva compiled this Vedic literature \[*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*****\], which is in relation to the Supreme Truth.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.7.6)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are Śrīla Vyāsadeva’s ideal representative, since you, following the order of your guru, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, have re-broadcast the message of Godhead to the entire world. In fact, you’ve distributed it more widely than anyone else before you. You not only presented the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Bhagavad-gītā, Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* and stacks of other literatures in English and dozens of other languages, but you also inspired thousands of your followers to deliver these literatures to people’s very doorsteps. The effects of this have been extraordinary.

Now millions of people all over the world have received—by your arrangement—*Kṛṣṇa* book, the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Light of the Bhāgavata, Śrī Īśopaniṣad, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* or one of the scores of other books that you miraculously produced. Each one of your books is a Deity of Kṛṣṇa, glowing like the sun and full of potency to reveal the Personality of Godhead to any sincere soul. Your books have already benefited untold numbers of people and will continue to do so as your followers carry on your work.

Even now, millions of your books lie like seeds waiting to germinate. They’re in office drawers, home bookshelves, hospital waiting rooms, coffee shops, motels, and automobile glove compartments, ready to sprout when watered by the interested gaze of some sincere soul searching for the purpose of life.

Recently, we met a young man—only twenty-two years old—whose mother bought a *Kṛṣṇa* book in 1970. Feeling an attraction for the book, she put it on her bookshelf, where it sat for nearly thirty-three years. A year ago, when her son began to search for God, she remembered the book, took it down from the shelf, and handed it to him. Now her son, Ben, inspired by that book, has structured his life around the practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and carries that very same *Kṛṣṇa* book with him wherever he goes, realizing that it is a divine manifestation of God in written form.

Without your books, Śrīla Prabhupāda, how many people would be able to discover the path back to Godhead? If you had not pressed your followers to distribute mass quantities of your books, who aside from a few rare *sādhus* in India or a handful of scholars in ivory towers would know about the teachings of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī and Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī? How many would be able to understand Lord Caitanya’s magnanimous gift?

Your books have saved the world.

Therefore, on this day of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, we, the members of ISKCON Silicon Valley, worship you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as Śrīla Vyāsadeva’s most merciful representative.

We pray on this day that we may become more organized for distributing your books to benefit the millions of people who live in Silicon Valley and beyond. We beg to always remember that our temple is *a base from which to go out to fight with Māyā by dropping thousands and millions of books into the laps of the conditioned souls*.

We also pray that we may fulfill your ultimate desire that we perfect our own lives by remaining always immersed in studying your books and singing the names of Lord Gaurāṅga, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana, and Śrī Śrī Lakṣmī-Narasiṁha, so that one day we will taste *kṛṣṇa-prema* for ourselves.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Oṁ Tat Sat*.

Your servants at ISKCON of Silicon Valley, California, USA.  
(written by Vaiśeṣika dāsa)

## Seweto

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurahari. All glories to Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā!

Over the last seven years since moving from Hillbrow to Soweto, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurahari and Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā have created many devotional opportunities for their devotees in Soweto to serve them. That includes spreading the message of Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda and maintaining the various programs that help our preaching develop.

At present, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our preaching includes Food for Life; the Sunday program; interacting with different youth groups; prison programs; and book distribution. Even though these programs are in place and we are trying our best to organize them for your pleasure, we are always faced with difficulties due to a lack of resources. Even thought the preaching goes on uninterrupted, the biggest problem for us is having no vehicle, which would help us pick up the vegetables from the market and distribute *prasādam* in and around Soweto. Still, Food for Life is happening twice a week, on Tuesdays and Wednesdays.

By your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have exciting opportunities and challenges before us here in ISKCON Soweto, and we are hoping that we will be able to overcome any difficulty that may arise. Preaching here in Soweto is undoubtedly very slow, and people in general take time to respond. Maybe this is due to the inexperience of the devotees and a lack of enthusiasm, but I am confident that if we strictly follow your instructions and learn about your pastimes, we will be victorious in the end in our effort to carry on your legacy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need your guidance at all times, and your association through your books, tapes, and senior disciples.

Thank you for being with us. For us to come this far is a sign that you are with us at all times. We thank you for everything and promise that we will improve our performance gradually as we go about our devotional service to Your Divine Grace.

Yours in the service of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā Mahārāṇī.

Your servants and aspiring servants at ISKCON Soweto, South Africa.  
(written by Mahāprabhu dāsa)

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you!

In one of the prayers to Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva, Prahlāda Mahārāja explains how He, the Lord, had sent him into this material world to exhibit the behavior of a pure devotee. And the essence of his behavior was to tolerate any inconvenience in this world for the sake of saving the conditioned souls, having full faith in the Lord’s protection. Lord Caitanya also descended to demonstrate what surrender to Kṛṣṇa means in this Kali-yuga, and Lord Nityānanda Prabhu’s mission was to reveal the glories of Lord Caitanya by very enthusiastically inducing everybody to accept Lord Gaurāṅga’s mercy.

And you in turn have demonstrated how to follow and surrender to Lord Caitanya by accepting the somewhat aggressively compassionate mood of Lord Nityānanda and attracting the fallen people of the modern times, as well as the times to come.

When some devotee indicated that you were in the mood of Prahlāda Mahārāja, you very humbly confirmed that. And all those who have encountered you, either physically or through your books or your representatives, can confirm the same. Your compassion is unlimited, like that of Lord Nityānanda, and it has manifested in many ways. Our ISKCON Society and the BBT were both created by your great and compassionate endeavor to make sure that the mercy of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi will be available for thousands of years. And it was the same compassion that made you request your followers to cooperate to continue spreading the *saṅkīrtana* mission after you left this world.

When Columbus took part in a competition to see who could make an egg stand on its end, he simply flattened one end by cracking it a little and then made it stand easily. Others said anybody could have done that. But it was he who did it. And it was you who actually risked your life to come to us in the West and give us Kṛṣṇa. What brought Kṛṣṇa consciousness west was not simply the principle of guru, nor even a representative of a spiritual tradition of Vaiṣṇavism: it was you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you, the person. Therefore, how can we leave your service? We simply want to follow you by trying to fulfill your desires by way of serving cooperatively in this ISKCON society, which is the instrument by which you serve your Guru Mahārāja.

You have given us and the world everything needed: *ācāra* and *pracāra*—a perfect example and perfect instruction. Please, therefore, bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we can follow in your footsteps, thus taking advantage of your mercy so that we can assist others to take the same advantage.

Your servants at the ISKCON’s center in Stockholm, Sweden.

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Today is Vyāsa-pūjā. It is a day to reflect. Various thoughts arise in our mind, feelings well up in our heart, and we become tossed around in a sea of sentiment. From one moment to the next we may be rendered sad, happy, remorseful, depressed, elated, grateful, proud, humble, confident, and crass. Emotions compete, conflict, become mixed, rise and fall like waves. Ultimately I tend to feel overwhelmed by two dominant themes, that of disgust and anger—directed both inward and outward; a miserable mix of hopelessness and loathing, overborne by a bitter determination arising out of resentment and contempt. It is complex. It is confusing. It is necessary.

I hate that my desire to serve you is not very strong. We are certainly a long way from being completely surrendered to your instructions and the unequivocal execution of your mission. The test of true love is not even on the horizon. Rather we are still struggling with the basic issues of loyalty, gratitude, and “proper theistic conduct.” If our movement is struggling, it reflects only our own insufficiency. We talk much about strategic planning, calculating certain actions to ensure a certain result, while effectively avoiding the obvious imperative to simply surrender more to you and your example. As you said about your own approach to service, “I simply follow my Guru Mahārāja blindly. I do not know the result.” Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, when will that day come when we simply take recourse to that ultimate strategy of simple unalloyed surrender to your lotus feet? When will we finally adopt that kind of strategic *devotional* intelligence, assume that kind of faith and fidelity?

Till now we are failing as disciples, still prevaricating in our commitment to you. Shamefully, we still harbor many independent ambitions, many plans for our personal sense gratification. Our faith is weak, our character low class. Only due to the merest sense of self-respect do we maintain some standard, continue going through the motions of so-called devotional service. Actually it is not out of love, but only out of various kinds of fear that we persevere in what we euphemistically call Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Considering things realistically, we must conclude that our position is not very good, hardly laudable, and certainly not praiseworthy. Still, depressing as it may be, such sobering reflections are far more healthy and appropriate than the fanciful sentiments and self-indulgent conceits of today’s ever-growing legion of cheap “Vaiṣṇavas.” If we cannot be very good disciples, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we at least pray to be saved from the kind of hypocrisy and cheating so prevalent now in the wake of your passing. I pray that, in comparison to you and your example, we may always see ourselves—and others—clearly, as we really are, and thereby keep feeling disgusted and angry. I actually like these sober sentiments. They mitigate our illusions. Counter our deceits. They are correct and proper. Actually they are a benediction, another symptom of your mercy.

Praying to always remain sober and realistic while struggling in your service,  
Your humble servants in Sydney, Australia.  
(written by Ātmārāma dāsa)

## Szeged

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances! All glories to your lotus feet, which give shelter to suffering souls!

How can I glorify you on this auspicious day? Your transcendental teachings and deeds are eternal. I will take one drop out of this boundless ocean.

Hungary is a little country, and Szeged, where I live, is a small city. We do not have many devotees in the temple, so this is a little community. New devotees come every year, having been connected to Kṛṣṇa consciousness somehow or other through your books, and your teachings touch their hearts. Maybe two years ago one young boy named John started to visit us regularly. Unfortunately, his financial situation was not so good; he could hardly find work, and there was no place for him to live. Also, his parents were very averse to Kṛṣṇa consciousness; they insisted that if he wanted to live at home, he would have to give up Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Against all difficulties he started to chant sixteen rounds soon, and at every opportunity he rendered some service. I was filled with admiration by his adherence.

And after some time he disappeared, and for almost one year we could not see him. I thought he had had some difficulty and stopped his practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Not long ago he appeared again and told us what had happened to him. His is a very elevating story. He told us that he had had many conflicts with his parents, but he never stopped his chanting and reading.

Then a big tragedy befell his family: his cousin, a young girl, became sick with cancer, and her days were numbered. One day her parents died in a car accident, and this young girl was then alone. The doctors did not want her to stay in the hospital, so her choice was either to go live with her relatives or be put in some government institution.

Bhakta John told his father to invite this girl and that he would take care of her. His father agreed, but he said, “No Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” This was the condition. John left his work and tried to help his cousin; of course, secretly he continued to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and offer his food. This was noticed by the girl, and she started to inquire about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. John instructed her with your teachings.
